Vouchers for Events FAQ

Thanks for your interest in Uber Vouchers. This voucher program has been designed to make transportation and food delivery arrangements for group events simpler and more convenient. Please review the information below if you have any questions about the program. You can head to event.uber.com to start setting up your event vouchers.

What are vouchers?

Vouchers can be used to provide riders or eaters with credit to use on the app for any event, like weddings, birthdays, conferences, and more. You can create vouchers for such events by visiting event.uber.com.

This program is currently only available in the United States. We hope to expand this offering to more cities and countries in the future.

I would like to create vouchers on behalf of my organization. Is there a separate process for that?

Yes, there is a separate product for organizations and business purposes. If you sign up for Uber for Business and create vouchers, you’ll enjoy higher spending and credit limits than consumer vouchers.

To create vouchers on behalf of your organization, please navigate to our Vouchers page for Uber for Business.

What kind of vouchers can I create?

You can create vouchers for either rides or meals.

With rides vouchers, you can cover credit for your guests’ Uber rides. You can also set restrictions on the dates (and times) that vouchers are valid, the value of the vouchers, and pickup and drop-off locations. Similarly for Uber Eats, you can restrict the amount covered by vouchers.

How can I distribute vouchers?

Once your vouchers are created, you’ll be able to copy the voucher link/code and share it with your guests. Guests then click the link to accept the voucher and add it to their Uber Account. Guests can also add the code directly to their Uber Account by navigating to their Wallet in the Uber/Uber Eats app, scrolling down to Vouchers and selecting Add Voucher code.

Note: These vouchers are for private distribution and personal use only. No marketing, resale, social media, or public messaging is permitted.

Can I customize or restrict my event vouchers?

Yes. When creating your event voucher, you can choose how many vouchers you’d like to create and the amount you’d like each voucher to cover. For rides vouchers, you can also restrict the pickup and drop-off locations.

How often will I be charged, and will I be charged for unused vouchers?

Your credit card will be charged at the end of every day, based on the value of vouchers used, and there is a $3,000 credit limit for all users. You will not be charged for unused vouchers.

How can I check if Uber will be available for my event?

  1. Open the Uber app and tap “Ride”.
  2. Enter the pickup and dropoff locations for your event.
  3. Once you’ve entered that information, you can check the wait time in the app and look for car icons on the map to see how many nearby drivers may be available.

We recommend performing this check in advance on the day of the week and at the time that your event is scheduled to get a sense of whether it’s a busy time on the platform.

Can I receive a report about the voucher usage and payments?

Since Events vouchers are primarily used for smaller events; receipts, commercial invoices, or CSVs are not available for report purposes. If you need to verify the voucher usage, you may contact the voucher recipients who can share a copy of their trip receipt.

If you’d require invoices and reports for vouchers used, you can sign up for Uber for Business here and use vouchers.

We no longer support the Apple Watch app. Please use the Uber mobile app to request rides. We’re sorry for any inconvenience.

Book an Uber ride through the Expedia Integration feature

You can now request an Uber ride directly from the Expedia mobile app. This new feature makes it easier to get to or from your hotel, airport, or any destination during your trip starting in the Expedia app.

Where can I find Uber in the Expedia app?

You can find the Uber option in several places within the Expedia mobile app:

  • On the Trip Overview page
  • On the Trip Item Details page
  • In push notifications related to your trip

Each option will show an Uber Ride card or button. When you tap it, you’ll be redirected to the Uber app to complete your booking.

How do I book a ride through Expedia?

To book your Uber with the Expedia Integration feature, you should:

  1. Open the Expedia mobile app and go to your booked trip.
  2. Tap the Uber Ride card or button.
  3. You’ll be redirected to the Uber app, where you can:
  • Sign in or create an Uber account
  • Choose your payment method
  • Confirm your pickup anddropoff locations
  • Complete your booking

What are the benefits of using the Expedia Integration feature?

This feature lets you easily request a ride to or from your hotel, airport, or any destination during your trip, all directly from the Expedia app.

Additionally, travelers who book an Uber ride through Expedia are eligible for a promotional discount of 5% off your next three rides within three days (up to $3 off each). The offer applies automatically after booking through Expedia; please note that the promotion cannot be combined with other Uber offers and that the terms may change at any time.

Where can I use this feature?

Right now, this feature is only available in the United States. You’ll see Uber as an option if both your pickup and dropoff locations are within the US.

Can I schedule a ride in advance?

At this time, only on-demand rides are available through Expedia. Reserve (scheduled) rides aren’t supported.

Who should I contact for help?

If you need help with your Uber trip (for example, a fare issue, driver concern, or lost item), please contact Uber Support through the Uber app. If you experience an issue accessing the Uber option in the Expedia app, please contact the Expedia Support.

Brand Health Tracker questions

Understanding Uber’s Research Surveys

At Uber, we’re always looking for ways to better understand and serve our users. Our survey program is one of the ways we listen to your feedback and identify trends that may impact how we evolve and improve our services.

Why You’re Receiving This Email

You may have received an email from Uber Research Insights (noreply@research.uber.com) inviting you to participate in a survey. This outreach is part of our official research conducted by Uber in partnership with Qualtrics, a trusted survey software provider.

Participation is entirely voluntary and helps us better understand how you feel about the Uber and Uber Eats platform.

Is This Email Legitimate?

Yes. If you received a survey invitation from Uber Research Insights (noreply@research.uber.com), it is a legitimate communication from Uber’s Research Insights team. These emails are sent exclusively for research purposes.

Is My Information Confidential?

Absolutely. Any information you provide in the survey is confidential and used strictly for research purposes. We may collect basic demographic data to better understand overall trends, but your personal data will not be shared or used for any other reason beyond improving our services.

Incentives & Gift Card Delivery

As a token of our appreciation, respondents who qualify and complete the full survey may receive a small incentive (e.g., a gift card). Here’s how it works:

  • Incentives are sent via email within 24 hours of completing the survey.
  • The gift card will be sent to the email address where you received the original survey invitation, unless you provided a different preferred email address during the survey.
  • Didn’t receive it? First, check your spam or junk folder.
  • If it’s been more than 24 hours and you still haven’t received your reward, please contact our support team at: uber-bht-support@qualtrics.com

How to Unsubscribe

If you no longer wish to receive this particular survey, you can unsubscribe by clicking the unsubscribe link included in the original survey invitation email. Once unsubscribed, you will no longer receive future communications related to this survey.

Delegate profiles FAQ

Riders with a business profile on their app can delegate access to the business profile to other designated Uber users. Delegates can remotely request rides on the rider’s behalf and manage post-trip support requests in the app. Both the rider and delegate receive in-app notifications, can track the trip in real-time, and can access past trip details and receipts.

Delegate profiles are a good solution for busy riders who need help managing their travel schedules, such as executive assistants (delegate) supporting executives (rider) within their organization.

Delegate profiles are available:

  • Exclusively for rides
  • Only for business profiles (not personal profiles)

To add a delegate for a business profile that is linked to an Uber for Business organization’s travel program:

  1. Open your Wallet in the app and go to Ride Profiles
  2. Find the business profile associated with your organization and select Add a delegate to your profile
  3. Select Add a delegate
  4. Read and confirm the Terms of Service
  5. Enter the business email of the user you want to add. This user must have an email address with your organization and have an active Uber account that is also linked to the Uber for Business travel program of your organization.

Delegates will receive an invite email and push notification, and your business profile will be automatically added to their Wallet.

To add a delegate for a business profile that is not linked to an Uber for Business organization’s travel program:

  1. Open your Wallet in the app and go to Ride Profiles
  2. Find the relevant business profile and select Add a delegate to your profile
  3. Select Add a delegate
  4. Read and confirm the Terms of Service
  5. Fill out the name and phone number of the user you want to add. This user must have an active Uber account.

Delegates will receive an invite email and push notification, and your business profile will be automatically added to their Wallet.

Payment methods

Debit cards and credit cards are the only accepted payment methods for trips requested on your behalf by a delegate. Delegates can’t change the payment method once a trip is requested. To update the payment method, the rider must contact us directly.

All requests placed by delegates use the default payment method associated with the rider’s business profile. Delegates can’t access the rider’s full payment details. They can only view the last 4 digits of the card and can’t make any changes to the payment method.

Note that if your delegate requests a trip in which the charges exceed your organization’s spending limit for your travel program, if any, the overage will be charged to your secondary payment method on your business profile.

Delegates can request trips for riders in certain markets and locations only. Check the app for availability.

Delegation Terms of Service

For reference, these are the Delegation Terms of Service that you agreed to when adding your delegate.

By sharing access to your business profile, you agree that:

  • The delegate can charge rides only to any credit or debit card on your business profile, including any amounts above your organization’s spending limit. They can request, change, and cancel rides on your behalf.
  • The delegate can access trip details and receipts for all rides on your business profile. They can also access your real-time trip location only for trips they have requested on your behalf.
  • You’ll obtain permission to share the delegate’s personal information with Uber and your organization.
  • You’re responsible for all delegate activity on your business profile and you remain subject to Uber’s general Terms of Use.
  • You may remove the delegate at any time in your profile settings.
  • Your delegate won’t be able to request trips for you in certain markets or locations. They’ll see a message in the app when trying to request in an unavailable location.

When agreeing to the Delegation Terms of Service, you also acknowledge the Privacy Notice.

Route Share FAQ

What is Route Share?

Route Share is a new shared rides product that operates on fixed routes and schedules with designated pick-up and drop-off locations. It offers an optimized route with minimal detours, allowing you to save money by walking a short distance before and after your trip.

You can reserve (one seat per booking up to seven days in advance through the Uber app.

Fares are fixed based on the route, and payment must be made via the Uber App. Cash payments are not accepted, and boarding is only possible with a prior reservation.

How to schedule a Route Share ride

  1. Make sure you’re using the latest version of the Uber app.
  2. Enter your destination, select the Route Share option, review your fare, select your preferred pick-up timing and then select Request.
  3. You’ll see the information about your trip in the app: driver information and pickup spot. At this point, your seat has been reserved. You can track it on the way to your pickup spot.
  4. Walk to the pickup spot you see on the map and make sure you arrive before your ride gets to the pickup spot. The driver will only wait 2 minutes at the pickup spot.
  5. We’ll drop you off at the location confirmed in your original booking. You’ll see walking directions to your final destination in the app.
  6. Enjoy your ride!

Can I pre-book?

Yes, you can pre-book any time from one week before the requested time and date. You also have the option of booking more than one ride throughout the whole week.

Why can’t I see the Route Share option in my app?

The ride type will only be available during commute hours and along certain routes in the city. If you don’t see the option, it means you are not on a serviceable Route Share area or you are outside booking hours.

How many seats can I book?

Route Share requests are limited to a single seat per ride. This policy is in place to make sure we have room for other co-riders who may join your trip.

Women Driver Preference Support

How will it work?

We’re adding new options to the app to help women riders and drivers match with each other.

  • Riders can choose Women Drivers when requesting or reserving a trip or set a preference to increase their chances of getting matched with a woman driver when requesting an UberX or Uber Electric trip.
  • Drivers can toggle on Women Rider Preference in their app settings and increase their chances of matching with women riders. They can toggle this option on or off whenever they want.

Who can use these new options?

These new preferences were designed for women riders and drivers. For riders, we’ll use technology to try to determine their gender based on their first name. For drivers, we’ll use the gender information on their government-issued identification. If a rider’s or driver’s gender in the Uber app is incorrect, they can update it:

  • Riders can update it by going to Account, then Settings, then selecting their name at the top and updating the Personal info section.
  • Drivers can update their gender in the settings of Uber’s Driver app by going to Menu > Settings > Manage Uber account > Personal Info > Gender.

Does this guarantee that women riders and drivers will be matched with another woman?

While we’ll do our best to match riders and drivers based on their preference when these options are selected, we can’t guarantee a match every time. Because there are fewer women drivers on the platform than men drivers, availability may be limited and can vary by time and location.

Where are these features available?

In the US, we’re currently piloting Women Preferences for riders and drivers in select cities, starting with Detroit, Los Angeles, and San Francisco. Women Rider Preference for drivers is already live in 40 countries, and we’re testing the suite of rider-focused Women Preferences in a handful of countries around the world.

Can riders and drivers change their preference at any time?

Yes, riders and drivers can turn their preference on or off at any time, as many times as they’d like. If they’re not getting trip options that meet their needs, they can update their preference at any point to explore different options.

What does this mean for men riders and drivers?

Their Uber experience won’t change; men riders will still be able to request trips and be matched with both men and women, and men drivers will continue to be able to accept any trip they’d like that they get offered.

Can transgender and non-binary riders and drivers use Women Preferences?

This feature is available only to riders and drivers whose gender is set to Woman in the Uber app or the Driver app.

Will this increase wait times for riders?

Riders using Women Preferences may experience longer wait times if women drivers are unavailable or farther away. If so, the rider can choose to wait, reserve a ride with a woman driver for a later time, or choose a ride with any available driver that may not match their preference.

Can women riders bring guests with them?

Yes, women riders can bring guests with them when requesting a trip with a woman driver. We ask that the woman rider requesting the trip always be in the vehicle with their guest riders. However, if a woman rider chooses Women Drivers when requesting a trip and brings a man rider, the woman driver can cancel the trip.

Will there be a difference in the price for Women Drivers trips?

While we aim to keep Women Drivers trips priced comparably to similar trips, all Uber trip prices are influenced by a variety of factors—including time, distance, driver availability, and demand. If a woman driver is farther from the pickup location than a man driver, or demand is higher than usual, the price of the trip may vary.

Women Rider and Driver Preference Availability

Women Rider Preference for drivers is already live in 40 countries, and we’re testing the suite of rider-focused Women Preferences in a handful of countries around the world.

In the US, Women Preferences for drivers is available in all cities nationwide.

Lost item

Forgot something in an Uber? We will help you find it!

Forgot something in an Uber? We will help you find it!

We encourage riders to make a habit of ensuring they have all their belongings with them before hopping out of an Uber. However, we understand that sometimes items are left behind accidentally, so we’re here to help you track down an item you may have left in a car. Here is how:

How do I contact my driver for a lost item?

The easiest way to recover a lost item is to contact your driver directly. To do so:

  1. Tap on Activity at the bottom of your Uber App
  2. Select the trip on which you lost your item
  3. Tap on Find Lost Item
  4. Tap on I need to contact my driver about a lost item
  5. Enter your contact number
  6. Tap on Call (Please note that the number provided would be connected to the driver’s number immediately)

I am unable to connect with my driver for a lost item

If you are unable to connect with the driver partner for an item, please raise a concern through the in-app Help section with the correct trip in reference. To do so:

  1. Tap on Activity at the bottom of your Uber App
  2. Select the trip on which you lost your item
  3. Tap on Find Lost Item
  4. Tap on I need to contact my driver about a lost item
  5. Tap No when asked ‘Were you able to contact the driver?’
  6. Select the item you forgot in your Uber trip and tap Submit Our team will be happy to assist and share an update with you usually within 24 hrs.

I forgot my registered phone in an Uber

You can contact us through the following options. To do so:

Sign in to help.uber.com on the web (not in the app) and call your driver by following the steps Go to help.uber.com and sign in with your Uber account on web.

  1. Click the Riders tile
  2. Click Help with a trip, then select the trip where you lost your phone
  3. Click I lost an item or Find lost item
  4. Click Contact driver about a lost item
  5. Enter a phone number you can currently use and click Submit.
  6. The system automatically calls that number and connects you to your driver through an anonymous phone line. There’s no separate Call button on the web.

If you are unable to sign in via help.uber.com, please reach out to us via the help section from a friend or relative’s Uber app. To do so please visit this page.

Please reach out to us via help.uber.com (This does not require the rider to be logged in) on the web (not in the app).

  1. Click on the Riders tile
  2. Click on Account
  3. Scroll down and click on I lost my phone in Uber

We request you share the necessary information such as the name and contact number of the registered account through which the trip was requested and an alternate number to reach out to the rider. Once done, our team will be happy to assist and share an update usually within 24 hours.

Other Information

We do our best to facilitate the return of lost items and help you connect with your driver. However, it is important to note that drivers are independent contractors, and hence, Uber and drivers do not bear responsibility for an item left in the vehicle after a trip ends.

In case your driver denies possession and you are certain that you left the item in the vehicle, you can always proceed legally and we’ll comply with the required information through appropriate channels.

Our privacy policy restricts us from sharing details about your driver. However, the trip receipt contains the driver’s license plate number and Name.

To protect your personal information such as your phone number, each time you contact the driver through the Uber app, your number is masked.

Can we help with anything else?

I had an issue with my package delivery

A few things to note before contacting us:

  • If your package hasn’t been delivered yet, you can call or message the driver in the app.
  • Drivers are free to cancel your request for any reason, including, for example, if your package was too heavy, too large for their vehicle, not packaged securely, or contained a prohibited item.
  • If the person receiving the package wasn’t available, the driver may try to contact you in the app to resolve the issue or coordinate a return. You’ll be responsible for costs associated with returning the package to you if necessary.
  • If your package was damaged during delivery and you would like to seek a refund of the delivery cost, you must submit a photo and description of the damage within three business days following the delivery date. Uber does not maintain insurance for packages.

For faster help, select your delivery issue below:

For other delivery issues, fill out the form below:

Map issue

Fix a business or landmark issue on Uber Maps

At Uber, we’re committed to delivering the most accurate and reliable maps for your convenience. If you’ve noticed incorrect information related to a business or local landmark on our maps, we want to hear from you! The more details you provide, the faster we can update and improve our map data. Follow the instructions below to report any errors or discrepancies.

Reporting business or landmark issues

  1. Go to the map problem reporting tool
  2. Select the issue type
  3. Enter the address or use the pin to mark the location of the issue
  4. Select Confirm location
  5. Add notes to help us understand the issue (provide as much detail as possible)
  6. Choose Submit

You can find types of business or landmark issues to report and what to include for each issue below. Thanks for helping to improve Uber Maps!

Business information is incorrect

If a business on Uber Maps has outdated or incorrect information (such as the wrong name, address, or contact details) let us know so we can fix it. Common issues include businesses that have moved, changed names, or closed but still appear on the map, or businesses with incorrect details like phone numbers or operating hours. By reporting these discrepancies, you help ensure that Uber Maps stays accurate and up to date, improving navigation for both drivers and riders.

When reporting this issue, please include the business name, correct address, and any other relevant updates.

Business moved/relocated

If you’ve noticed that a business has moved to a new location but the old address still appears on Uber Maps, you can help us correct it. Common issues include businesses that have changed locations but haven’t had their map information updated. Reporting these changes ensures that riders and drivers can navigate to the correct location.

When reporting this issue, please provide the business’s name, its old address, its new/updated location, and any other relevant updates.

Business/place is permanently closed or no longer exists

If a business has permanently closed or a place no longer exists but still appears on Uber Maps, you can help us keep our data current by reporting it. Common issues include businesses that have shut down, demolished buildings, or landmarks that no longer exist. Reporting these closures ensures that riders and drivers aren’t misled by outdated map information.

When reporting this issue, please provide the full name, address, and any other relevant details about the missing location so we can add it to our maps promptly.

Business’s building footprint is missing or incorrect

If you’ve encountered a location on Uber Maps where a building’s footprint is missing or incomplete, we want to address it. This can include businesses, homes, or landmarks that don’t appear accurately on the map. Whether the building outline is absent, incorrect, or outdated, reporting these issues helps improve map precision for riders and drivers alike.

Access road is incorrect

If a landmark or business on Uber Maps is shown with the wrong access road or entrance, it can confuse drivers and riders. This issue often occurs when a location’s main entrance is on a different road than what’s displayed on the map. Whether the entrance has changed or the map data is outdated, it’s important to report these errors to improve navigation accuracy.

When reporting this issue, please include the correct road or entrance information, along with the place’s name and address, and any other relevant updates.

Business/place is missing

If you’ve discovered a business, landmark, or other important location that’s missing from Uber Maps, we want to know. Common issues include new businesses or landmarks that haven’t been added yet or entire areas that are underrepresented on the map. Reporting missing places helps improve the accuracy of Uber Maps for everyone.

When reporting this issue, please provide the full name, address, and any other relevant details about the missing location so we can add it to our maps quickly.

Name of the business/place changed

If a business has recently changed its name but the old name still appears on Uber Maps, you can help keep our data accurate by reporting the update. This is common for businesses that have rebranded or undergone ownership changes. By reporting the correct name, you ensure that drivers and riders can easily find the updated business.

When reporting this issue, please include the business’s old name, new name, and its current address to ensure the change is made quickly and accurately.

Other map issues

If you’ve encountered an issue on Uber Maps that doesn’t fit into any of the categories listed, we still want to hear from you! This can include problems like incorrect road layouts, outdated neighborhood information, or any other discrepancies that affect the map’s accuracy. By reporting these issues, you help us continually improve Uber Maps for everyone.

When reporting these issues, please provide a detailed description of the problem along with any relevant addresses or landmarks to assist in resolving the issue efficiently.

Can we help with anything else?

Fix an incorrect address on Uber Maps

If you’ve noticed an incorrect address on Uber Maps—whether it’s a home, business, or landmark—you can help us fix it. Issues like wrong house numbers, misplaced pins, or incorrect street names can cause navigation problems for riders and drivers. By reporting an inaccurate address, you ensure that everyone can reach their destination smoothly. Follow the steps below to submit the correct address information, including details like street name, house number, or any other relevant updates to help us make the fix promptly.

Reporting address issues

  1. Go to the map problem reporting tool
  2. Select the issue type
  3. Enter the address or use the pin to mark the location of the issue
  4. Select Confirm location
  5. Add notes to help us understand the issue (provide as much detail as possible)
  6. Choose Submit

You can find types of address issues to report and what to include for each issue below. Thanks for helping to improve Uber Maps!

Address is missing or doesn’t exist on Uber Maps

If an address you’re looking for is missing from Uber Maps, or it doesn’t exist on the platform at all, you can report this issue to help us improve map accuracy. Whether it’s a newly developed property, a home, or a business, adding the missing address ensures riders and drivers can easily locate it.

When reporting this issue, please provide the full address and any additional information to help us add it to our maps as soon as possible.

Address is in the wrong location

If an address is listed on Uber Maps but appears in the wrong location, such as being pinned to a nearby street or the wrong side of a block, this can confuse riders and drivers. Reporting an address with the wrong placement ensures that the correct location is displayed on the map.

When reporting this issue, please include the correct address and details about the location’s accurate placement to help us resolve the issue.

Address is on the wrong road

Sometimes, an address may be listed on the wrong road, especially in areas with multiple access points or complex road layouts. If you’ve noticed this issue, reporting it helps update Uber Maps with the correct road for accurate navigation.

When reporting this issue, please provide the correct road name and any other relevant information to assist us in making the fix.

Address information is incorrect

If you’ve found that the address listed has inaccurate details, such as an incorrect house number, street name, or postal code, you can report the issue. Correcting these errors improves map accuracy for riders and drivers.

When reporting this issue, please include the correct address details when submitting your report to ensure quick updates.

Other address issues

If the address issue you’re experiencing doesn’t fall under the categories listed, you can still report it. This might include problems like incorrect intersection information, errors in rural areas, or any other address discrepancies.

When reporting these issues, please include the correct address details when submitting your report to ensure quick updates.

Can we help with anything else?

Fix road information on Uber Maps

If you’ve encountered incorrect or outdated road information on Uber Maps (such as a road that’s missing, mislabeled, or inaccurately placed) you can report it to help us improve navigation accuracy. Common road-related issues include wrong road names, incorrect road placements, or roads that are closed but still appear on the map. By reporting these errors, you help ensure smoother trips for both riders and drivers. Follow the steps below to submit details about the road issue and help us update the map accordingly.

Reporting road information issues

  1. Go to the Map Problem Reporting Tool.
  2. Select the issue type
  3. Enter the address or use the pin to mark the location of the issue
  4. Select Confirm location
  5. Add notes to help us understand the issue (provide as much detail as possible)
  6. Attach photos (optional but highly recommended)
  7. Choose Submit

You can find types of road information issues to report and what to include for each issue below. Thanks for helping to improve Uber Maps!

Road is missing or doesn’t exist

If a road is missing from Uber Maps or a new road hasn’t been added yet, reporting it ensures that all routes are accurately reflected.

When reporting this issue, please provide the correct road name and location to help us update the map quickly and improve navigation for riders and drivers.

Road name is incorrect

Sometimes roads may appear on the map but with the wrong name, leading to confusion for navigation. If you’ve spotted an incorrect road name, report it along with the correct name and relevant location details to help us update it accurately on Uber Maps.

Road is in the wrong location

If a road is mapped incorrectly—whether it’s in the wrong position, direction, or connected to the wrong streets—you can report the issue to have it corrected.

One-way road issue

If you’ve encountered a one-way road that is incorrectly marked on Uber Maps—either as a 2-way road or with the wrong direction—this can cause serious navigation issues. Reporting the problem helps ensure drivers follow the correct routes.

When reporting this issue, please provide the road’s name, location, and the correct one-way direction to help us update it accurately.

Private road issue

If a private road is incorrectly shown as accessible on Uber Maps, or if a public road is mistakenly marked as private, this can confuse drivers. Reporting these issues helps avoid unnecessary detours.

When reporting this issue, please provide details about the road’s name, location, and its correct status to ensure proper updates.

Road can’t be driven on

If a road appears on Uber Maps but is inaccessible to vehicles (for example, pedestrian-only streets, restricted areas), this can lead to route errors for drivers. Let us know if there are roads you can’t drive on that are incorrectly marked.

When reporting this issue, please include the road’s name and details about the restriction to help us correct it.

Road should be open

If a road is marked as closed on Uber Maps but is actually open for use, reporting it helps keep our maps accurate for drivers and riders. Whether it’s a road that has reopened after construction or an incorrectly closed route, we want to help.

When reporting this issue, please provide the road’s name, location, and details to assist us in updating it.

Road should be closed

If a road that should be closed due to construction, safety reasons, or any other restrictions is still shown as open on Uber Maps, it can cause routing issues.

When reporting this issue, please include the road name and details about the closure to ensure accurate navigation.

Turn should be allowed

If a turn is incorrectly restricted on Uber Maps, such as a legal turn that’s marked as prohibited, this can cause unnecessary detours. Report the issue to help us update turn permissions and improve routing.

When reporting this issue, please provide the road names and details of the intersection where the turn should be allowed and the name of the road to turn on.

Can’t make suggested turn

If Uber Maps suggests a turn that isn’t possible due to restrictions or road design (for example, a no-left-turn or a physically blocked intersection), report the problem to prevent future routing errors.

When reporting this issue, please include details about the road and intersection where the turn is incorrectly suggested.

Other road issues

For any road-related issues that don’t fit into the categories listed, you can still report the problem. This might include incorrect road types (for example, a one-way street marked as 2-way), intersections, or any other discrepancies.

When reporting these issues, please provide a detailed description of the issue along with location information to ensure it’s addressed.

Other payment support

Currency Preferences

How does Preferred Currency Pricing work?

In markets where Preferred Currency Pricing is available, Uber may assign your home currency as your preferred currency by default. Preferred Currency Pricing allows riders to see pricing in their familiar home currency — avoiding the hassle of doing currency conversion math when requesting a ride. A conversion fee applies when you choose to pay in your preferred currency instead of the local currency. The applicable fee may vary depending on the preferred currency selected and will be shown before you confirm your ride.

Note: Preferred Currency Pricing is not currently available for Australian riders travelling overseas. For these trips, prices will be shown in the local currency of the country or region you’re in.

You may update your preference under “Preferred Currency” in your Uber Wallet settings at any time to pay in the local currency and avoid Uber’s conversion fee

What is the fee charged for Uber’s Preferred Currency Pricing?

If you choose to pay in a preferred currency instead of the local currency, Uber will charge a conversion fee based on the preferred currency used for the transaction. The fee is calculated as a percentage of the total trip fare, including all applicable taxes and fees required by law, but excluding any tips.

The applicable conversion fees are:

  • USD, CAD, EUR, GBP, JPY, BRL, ARS, PEN, UYU, PYG, CHF, ILS: 1.5%
  • BRL: 1.5%, plus IOF of 3.5% charged by the payment processor
  • COP: 0.4%
  • KRW: 1.0%
  • MXN: 0.4%

When is Preferred Currency Pricing available to me?

Preferred Currency Pricing is available only for on-demand rides such as UberX, UberXL, Uber Black, and Uber Green. Please note that rides like Rentals, Reserve, Scheduled Rides, those with 3rd-party partners, and Uber Eats or Delivery orders are not eligible.

Preferred Currency Pricing is not available for your business profile that is linked to your employer or similar organization, but you’ll still be able to manually opt-in to Preferred Currency Pricing for eligible trips on your personal profile.

What if I choose to pay in the local currency?

Prices will be shown in the currency of the country or region you’re in. Your payment provider may charge foreign currency transaction fees. We recommend checking the terms of your credit/debit card or the selected payment method to learn more about fees that may apply to foreign transactions, to decide which option is best for you.

How can I opt out of Preferred Currency Pricing or change my currency preferences?

You may choose to opt out of Preferred Currency Pricing at any time. Simply go to your Wallet, select Set preferred currency, and choose Always pay in local currency.

In what countries or regions is Preferred Currency Pricing available?

Preferred Currency Pricing is available for rides taken outside your home country, depending on your home currency and the country where you’re riding.

If your preferred currency is one of the following, you’ll see ride prices in your home currency when riding in these countries or regions:

  • ARS (Argentine peso) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • BRL (Brazilian real) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • CAD (Canadian dollar) — United States, Eurozone, United Kingdom, Mexico, Brazil and Japan
  • CHF (Swiss franc) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • COP (Colombian peso) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • EUR (Euro) — United States, Canada, United Kingdom, Mexico, Brazil and Japan
  • GBP (British pound) — United States, Canada, Eurozone, Mexico, Brazil and Japan
  • ILS (Israeli shekel) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • JPY (Japanese yen) — United States, Canada, Eurozone, United Kingdom, Mexico and Brazil
  • KRW (South Korean won) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • MXN (Mexican peso) — United States, Canada, Eurozone, United Kingdom
  • PEN (Peruvian sol) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • PYG (Paraguayan guaraní) — United States, Canada, Mexico
  • USD (US dollar) — Canada, Eurozone, United Kingdom, Mexico, Brazil and Japan
  • UYU (Uruguayan peso) — United States, Canada, Mexico

Are there any restrictions on payment types?

Preferred Currency Pricing is only available when paying by credit card, debit card, or a digital wallet (e.g., Apple Pay or Google Pay). The following payment options are never eligible, regardless of currency: Split Fare, Uber Cash, Uber Money, Cash, Gift Cards, Alipay, Venmo, and Zaakpay. Additional restrictions apply for certain home currencies:

  • Brazilian Real (BRL): Only credit cards are supported. Debit cards, Apple Pay, Google Pay, and PIX are not currently available.
  • Argentine Peso (ARS) and Paraguayan Guaraní (PYG): Apple Pay and Google Pay are not currently available.
  • Colombian Peso (COP), Peruvian Sol (PEN), and Uruguayan Peso (UYU): American Express cards, Apple Pay, and Google Pay are not currently available.
  • Panamanian balboa (PAB): American Express cards, Apple Pay, and Google Pay are not currently available for trips outside the United States.
  • Canadian Dollar (CAD): Google Pay is not currently available for trips taken in Mexico.

You can change your payment method at any time. If the new payment method is eligible for Preferred Currency Pricing, your preferred currency will continue to apply. If the new payment method is not eligible, your trip will automatically be priced in the local currency instead.

Can I switch my currency selection during the trip?

Once your ride is confirmed, you may only switch the payment method you would like to use for the trip, not the currency selection. Once you make your currency selection, charges on any new eligible payment method added to your wallet will be processed in your updated preferred currency.

What about tips?

Uber’s Preferred Currency Pricing currency conversion fee described above will only apply to your trip fare at the time of your trip request, and will not be applied to any tip you would like to pay your driver. Tips will be charged in your preferred currency for the corresponding transaction, and the conversion fee will not be applied.

Cancellation fees, clearing of arrears, and changing destinations will all be charged in the same currency as charged on the corresponding transaction.

What exchange rate applies to Preferred Currency Pricing?

For all preferred currency transactions, the mid-market exchange rate is applied with no additional. You may view the applicable rate in the “Fare breakdown” screen by selecting the tooltip (ℹ icon) on the “confirm details” page before requesting your ride.

Editing a tip amount

If you tipped the driver during the trip, you can edit your tip amount in the app until the trip ends. After that, you can only add to the tip you initially gave. you can add a tip for a Driver up to 90 days after the trip (depending on your location). You won’t be able to adjust the tip amount.

To edit the tip amount (during the trip):

  1. Open the Uber app.
  2. Tap the white screen to expand the trip details.
  3. Select “Edit” next to the rating.
  4. Select or enter the amount you want to tip and tap “Save.”

To add to a tip (up to 90 days after the trip):

  1. Open the Uber app.
  2. Tap the menu icon and then “Your trips.”
  3. Select the trip you want to increase the tip amount for.
  4. Tap “Add to your tip.”
  5. Enter the additional amount you want to tip and tap “Set tip.”

To learn how to tip a driver, see this article.

How do I get a copy of the receipt for this trip?

Your Uber account contains a history of all the trips you’ve taken and the receipts. To review:

  1. Select “Activity” from the app menu.
  2. Select a completed trip.
  3. View the trip route, pickup location, and destination.
  4. To see a full price breakdown, tap “Receipt”.

When viewing the receipt, scroll to the bottom and select “Download PDF” if you want to generate a file of this receipt, or “Resend email” if you prefer to receive this receipt in your registered email address.

If you don’t find the receipts in your inbox, check your spam and junk folders. To verify your email address, select “Account” from the main screen and go to “Settings”. Tap your name at the top of the screen, select your email address to change it (if necessary), and tap “Save”.

Note: For trips requested through our partners (e.g., WeChat), you can find the receipt in the Uber app.

How to clear a pending amount

If you’ve already attempted to do this in your app and are still unable to clear the outstanding balance, please follow the link below.

If you’re seeing an outstanding balance on your account, it could be because a transaction from your payment method was unsuccessful. This can happen when a payment method has insufficient funds or a technical issue caused a transaction to fail.

When an unsuccessful transaction occurs, you may be unable to request, schedule a ride or tip your driver.

You can clear this outstanding amount directly from the Uber app. Before requesting your next ride, the app will give you a prompt to choose a payment method for the charge and pay the balance. If your payment method is declined, you’ll need to update it or select a different one.

Temporary authorization holds

When you make a trip request, Uber may need to verify your payment method by issuing a temporary authorization hold.

At the start of a trip, Uber may place a temporary authorization hold for the upfront price of the trip on this payment method. It appears as a pending charge on your account’s payment method. This is a standard industry practice.

Authorization holds are not applied when you use Uber Cash to pay for your trip. If you would like to avoid an authorization hold, you can use Uber Cash as your payment method.

If this is a hold, why was I charged twice?

Uber voids the authorization hold after your trip is complete. Sometimes, an authorization hold isn’t processed by your bank at the same speed as the actual charge, leading your account balance to appear as if you were charged twice.

When you check the charge, you’ll see the hold stays in pending status, until it disappears from your account.

How long does it take for the hold to be reversed?

Uber reverses the hold immediately after your trip ends. This normally takes 3-5 business days to reflect in your account.

How can I see what I was actually charged?

You can always visit the Your Trips section in your Uber app and review the receipt from any previous trip. You’ll be able to see the amount, the payment method that was charged, and several other trip details you might be interested in reviewing.

American Express Platinum Card® and Centurion® Card Uber Cash Benefit

You can receive up to $200 in Uber Cash annually by adding your U.S. Consumer American Express Platinum Card® or Centurion® Card (“Amex Card”) as a payment method to your Uber account.

  • Uber Cash received through Uber with your Amex Card is distributed in monthly increments of $15, plus a $20 bonus in December (for $35 total in December)(the “Amex Benefit”). The Amex Benefit can be used for rides and orders in the U.S. when you select an Amex Card for your transaction.
  • The Amex Benefit will expire at the end of each month and won’t roll over to the following month.

When your Amex Benefit isn’t enough to cover the cost of your ride or order, the Amex Card in your Uber wallet selected for the transaction will be charged the difference. If Uber is unable to use the Amex Card in your Uber wallet to complete the transaction, the Amex Benefit will not be applied and another available payment method in your Uber wallet will be charged for the transaction. The Amex Benefit will not automatically apply to rides or orders when using a business profile. To use the Amex Benefit, you must toggle Uber Cash on in your business profile, which may be restricted based on your employer organization’s setting for such profile.

Follow these steps to redeem the Amex Benefit when requesting a ride or placing an order:

  1. Enter your destination in the Uber app or create an order in the Uber Eats app or Postmates App
  2. At checkout, select an American Express Card as the payment method for your ride or order and make sure Uber Cash is toggled on
  3. Request your ride or place your order

To find your Uber Cash balance

  1. Select Account in the bottom right corner of your app
  2. Select Wallet/Payment
  3. From there, you can also view your Amex Benefit history by selecting Uber balances

You’ll receive the Amex Benefit each month as long as your Amex Card remains eligible and is a payment method on your Uber account. If your eligible Amex Card is added to multiple Uber accounts, the account to which you first added the Amex Card will receive the associated Amex Benefit.

Adding an eligible Amex Card to your Uber account

  1. Go to the Wallet/Payment section of the Uber, Uber Eats, and/or Postmates apps
  2. Select Add Payment Method and enter your eligible Amex Card information

Basic Card Members on a Consumer Platinum Card or Centurion Account are eligible. Additional Centurion Cards are also eligible. You’ll receive $15 in Uber Cash each month (plus the $20 bonus in December) for each eligible Amex Card on your Uber account.

If you do not see this Uber Cash in your Uber Cash balance within 48 hours from the time you enroll, call the number on the back of your Card for assistance.

Switching which Uber account receives the Uber Cash Amex Benefit

  1. Delete the Amex Card from all Uber accounts that you no longer wish to receive the Amex Benefit
  2. Add the Amex Card as a payment method on the Uber account where you do wish to receive the Amex Benefit. If the Amex Card is already on this account, skip this step.

The Amex Benefit will switch accounts and apply to that new account starting on the 1st day of the next month. You can always re-add your eligible Amex Card to other Uber accounts after completing these steps if you want the users to be able to pay for Uber rides and orders with the Amex Card.

Further details will be available at uber.com/signaturesupport starting on May 7, 2026.

American Express Platinum Card® and Centurion® Card Uber Cash Benefit and Signature Support Terms and Conditions

Basic Card Members on a Consumer Platinum or Centurion account and Additional Centurion Members are eligible for $15 Uber Cash each month plus an additional $20 Uber Cash in December, and Signature Support for Amex, a premium level of phone customer service including 24/7 access to live customer support agents (“Amex Benefit”). To receive the Amex Benefit, you must have downloaded the latest version of the Uber, Uber Eats and/or Postmates apps and added your eligible Platinum Card or Centurion Card as a payment method in your Uber account. It may take up to 48 hours for the Amex Benefit to become available in your Uber account after your eligible Card has been added to your account. To redeem the Uber Cash received through the Amex Benefit, you must select an American Express Card as the payment method for your transaction and have Uber Cash toggled on. The Amex Benefit may only be used in the United States.

To be eligible for the Amex Benefit, your Card Account must not be canceled or past due. American Express reserves the right to suspend your eligibility for the Amex Benefit if we suspect any violation or abuse. Cards added to your Uber account through a third party or electronic wallet such as Apple Pay or PayPal will not be eligible to receive the Amex Benefit. An eligible Platinum Card or Centurion Card can only receive the Amex Benefit on one Uber account. If the same Card is added to multiple Uber accounts, only the first Uber account to which the Card is added will receive the Amex Benefit. The Uber Cash received through the Amex Benefit is found within your Uber Cash balance. If you do not see this Uber Cash in your Uber Cash balance within 48 hours from the time you enroll, call the number on the back of your Card for assistance.

To continue to receive the Amex Benefit, the details for your eligible Card must be kept up-to-date in your Uber account and your Uber, Uber Eats and/or Postmates app versions must be kept up-to-date. If your American Express Card details change, you will need to update your Card details in your Uber payment profile before the end of the current month to ensure you remain enrolled in the Amex Benefit, otherwise you may need to re-enroll to continue to receive your Amex benefit. The Uber App, and all other Uber products and services, including but not limited to Signature Support for Amex and Uber Cash, are governed by Uber’s terms and conditions. Any information you provide to Uber will be subject to Uber’s Privacy Notice available at 0 and Uber’s Terms of Use available at 1.

In order to use the Uber Cash received through the Amex Benefit, Uber Cash must be toggled on and an American Express Card must be selected as the payment method for your transaction.

The Amex Benefit may be applied to any services to which Uber permits Uber Cash to be applied. If your Uber Cash balance does not satisfy the cost of your ride or order, the Amex Card selected for the transaction will be charged for the difference.

The Amex Benefit will not apply to previous Uber transactions and cannot be used when paying with a family profile. Uber will apply Uber Cash received through the Amex Benefit at the point of sale and it will be displayed on your receipt.

Uber Cash received through the Amex Benefit expires at 11:59 PM Hawaii Standard Time on the last day of each calendar month. Unused balance in Uber Cash from your Amex Benefit will not carry over to the following month. Uber Cash received through the Amex Benefit will be applied for the month in which the transaction is completed. If a transaction is eligible for another promotion that you have added to your Uber account, the promotion will be applied before your Amex Benefit. Certain other types of balances in Uber Cash may be applied to the cost of your ride or order prior to your Amex Benefit in Uber Cash.

Signature Support for Amex can be accessed in the Account section of the Uber, Uber Eats, or Postmates apps via the Partner Rewards or Help pages.

American Express will share with Uber certain Card information, including Card type, for the purposes of benefit fulfillment and joint marketing campaigns (for example, exclusive Card Member offers). If you do not see the Uber Cash portion of the Amex Benefit in your Uber Cash balance by 5:00 PM Hawaii Standard Time on the first day of each calendar month, call the number on the back of your Card for assistance.

Can we help with anything else?

American Express Qualifying Corporate Card® Uber Cash Benefit

Eligibility requirements

Anyone with a US-issued American Express® Corporate Card—Green, Gold, or Platinum—is eligible. All you have to do is add your Corporate Card to your Uber Wallet and enroll in the new benefit to receive Uber Cash on eligible transitions.

After you’re enrolled, simply ride or order with an eligible American Express Corporate Card to begin earning Uber Cash.

To enroll:
  1. Add your American Express Corporate Card as a payment method in your Uber Wallet
  2. A window will pop up and the Card Member must select Start earning Uber Cash to enroll in the benefit
  3. If your eligible card is already in your Uber Wallet, navigate to your account and scroll down to Partner Rewards
  4. Select Uber Cash with your Amex Corporate Card, then Enroll Now
  5. A window will pop up and the Card Member must select Start earning Uber Cash to enroll in the benefit

Benefits summary

Uber Cash can be earned on amounts paid by Card Members enrolled in the benefit for Uber Eats orders, UberX Share, UberX, UberXL, WAV/Assist, Comfort, Courier, Pet, Select, Green, Black, and Black SUV trips, as well as tips to drivers or delivery people.

When you meet the eligibility requirements listed above and take a business ride with Uber or order with Uber Eats using your American Express® Corporate Card, you’ll earn the following Uber benefits:

  • Corporate Green Card Members will earn 3% Uber Cash on eligible spend when they take eligible rides for business and on Uber Eats business orders when booked and charged to their Corporate Green Card
  • Corporate Gold and Corporate Platinum Card Members will earn 5% Uber Cash on eligible spend when they take eligible rides for business and on Uber Eats business orders when booked and charged to their Corporate Platinum or Corporate Gold Card

Card Members won’t earn Uber Cash on:

  • Car rentals
  • Cancellation Fees
  • Portions of trips covered by a promotional value
  • Portions of trips covered by another user through Split Fare
  • External trip fees such as damage or Cleaning Fees
  • Upfront purchases of Uber Cash, credits, or subscription passes
  • Taxi, bike, and scooter rides

Uber Cash summary

Uber Cash is a payment option that can be used toward rides with Uber and orders with Uber Eats as well as bikes and scooters in the US.

Before requesting a trip, you can manage when you want to use your Uber Cash. To do this:

  1. Enter your destination
  2. Select your current payment method
  3. If you have a profile set up, select Change
  4. Turn Uber Cash on or off
  5. Return to the previous screen and request your ride

To check your Uber Cash balance, select the menu icon in your app and choose Wallet.

If at any time, you downgrade your American Express® Corporate Card, you’ll be eligible for benefits associated with the downgraded card. If you delete your business profile, you’ll no longer be eligible for the benefit. For more information on the benefit, please visit uber.com/u/amex-corp-card.

If you have specific questions about your American Express® Corporate Card or other American Express benefits, please call the number on the back of your American Express Card.

Managing your payment methods

Change payment method for this trip

You can change the payment method for trips within 30 days (60 days for valid business trips that fall under your company’s ride policy). However, this does not apply to business profile trips that do not fall under your company’s ride policy.

Additionally, the option to update the payment method for a family trip as the family organizer is currently unavailable. You’ll need to change the payment method selected for all trips in your Family profile, or adult family members in your Family profile can switch the payment method for the trip themselves.

To change your payment method after a trip:

  • Open the Uber app and go to Account
  • Select Help, then choose the trip you want to update
  • Select Help with a trip, then Other Payment support
  • Follow the prompts to change your payment method

To change your payment method during a trip:

  • In your app, select the white panel at the bottom of your screen
  • Select Switch next to the price and payment method
  • Select the correct payment method

You won’t be able to switch to or from Apple Pay, Google Pay, American Express reward points, or cash (if you’re in a city that accepts cash as a payment option).

Get Up to $120 Back on Uber One with Eligible American Express® Cards

Enjoy Uber One membership and receive up to $120 in statement credits each calendar year when you pay with an eligible U.S. Consumer American Express Platinum Card® or Centurion® Card. This perk applies to both monthly and annual Uber One membership purchases.

To take advantage of the perk, simply pay for your Uber One membership (subject to auto-renewal) with your eligible American Express® Card directly through Uber. The American Express perk is valid for Uber One memberships and Uber accounts in the U.S. (excluding Northern Mariana Islands, American Samoa, Guam, and U.S. Virgin Islands).

Eligible Cards

  • U.S. Consumer Platinum Card®
  • U.S. Consumer Centurion® Card

Important Notes:

  • Statement credits are only issued for the amount actually charged to the Card.
  • Credits will not apply to Uber One membership fees paid with Uber Cash, Uber One credits, discounts, or promotional offers.
  • Membership is auto-renewing. See Uber One Membership Terms and Conditions for full details.
  • To see the perk’s full Terms and Conditions, please visit here.

How to manage your Uber One membership

Follow these steps to manage your Uber One membership through the Uber app:

  1. Open the Uber, Uber Eats, or Postmates app.
  2. Tap your account icon in the bottom-right corner.
  3. Select Uber One.
  4. From this page, you can view your membership status, billing date, and manage payment methods.

To continue receiving this American Express Card perk:

  • Be sure to keep an eligible American Express Card on file as your default payment method for the Uber One membership. Ensure Uber Cash balances in your Uber or Uber Eats app are toggled off for membership payments to receive the maximum available statement credit.
  • If your Card details change (e.g., expired, replaced, or updated), update your payment method in the app before your next Uber One renewal day found in your Uber Membership Hub.

How to cancel your Uber One membership

You can cancel your Uber One membership at any time:

  1. Open the Uber or Uber Eats app.
  2. Tap your account icon, then select Uber One.
  3. Tap Manage Membership, then choose End Membership.

Keep in mind:

  • If you cancel your membership, you will stop earning statement credits.
  • Cancelling your Card or removing it from your Uber account does not automatically cancel your Uber One membership.
  • To stop recurring charges, you must cancel your Uber One membership directly within the app.

Learn more about cancellation policies in the Uber One Membership Terms and Conditions.

Frequently Asked Questions

Can I get the statement credit if an Additional Card Member pays for the membership?
  • For Platinum Card Members: Yes, the Uber One membership can be purchased by either the Basic Card Member or any Additional Card Members on the account. However, the total credit will not exceed $120 per calendar year per eligible Card Account.
  • For Centurion Members: Centurion Members and Additional Centurion Members are each eligible for up to $120 in total credits per calendar year. Additional Card Members, other than Additional Centurion Members on the Card Account, are not eligible for their own perk, and their purchases are not eligible for statement credits.
What if I change or replace my eligible American Express Card?

If your eligible Card is replaced, updated, or expires, you must remove the old Card and add the new eligible American Express Card as your payment method for Uber One in the Uber or Uber Eats app. Failure to do so may result in missed statement credits.

What counts as an eligible purchase?

Eligible purchases are Uber One membership fees charged directly by Uber to your eligible American Express Card. The credit will not apply to portions of the membership fee which are:

  • Paid with Uber Cash, Uber One credits, or other promotional offers.
  • Made using a third-party payment method such as Apple Pay or PayPal.
  • Discounted or part of a free trial.
What happens if I cancel my Uber One membership?

Once you cancel your Uber One membership, you will no longer earn statement credits. Removing your eligible Card from your Uber account does not cancel your subscription. Be sure to cancel directly through the Uber app to stop recurring charges.

Need help?

If you don’t see a statement credit within 8 weeks after an eligible transaction, please call the number on the back of your American Express Card.

How to request a WAV

WAV features vehicles that can transport riders traveling in motorized and other types of wheelchairs. Folding wheelchairs and other assistive devices may generally be transported in vehicles available through other Uber products.

To request a WAV vehicle in Toronto, please use the following steps:

  1. Enter your pickup and destination locations
  2. Slide up through the vehicle options until you reach “More” section
  3. Select “WAV”
  4. Tap on “CONFIRM WAV” to complete the request

Please note that WAV will cost the same as an UberX trip.

I can’t clear a pending amount

If a charge from Uber is declined by your payment method, you may be temporarily unable to request rides.

Your app will ask you to charge your outstanding balance to a payment method of your choice. If this payment method is declined, please add a new payment profile and try again. Reach out to your bank to ensure your payment method is valid.

You can also pay an outstanding balance on riders.uber.com. Here’s how:

  1. Select “Wallet” or “Payment” from the menu.
  2. Update your payment method(s).
  3. Choose “My Trips” from the menu to review and pay your balance.

If you’ve already attempted these steps and are still unable to clear the outstanding balance, please follow the link below.

It’s always recommended to check the spelling of your email and make sure you’ve entered your new phone number correctly.

I cannot add or delete payment methods

If you see any of the error messages below in your app, you won’t be able to request a ride until you update your payment method:

  • “Request Failed: Card Declined”
  • “Your payment method is invalid. Please update your billing settings”
  • “There was an error processing your request”

Update or add a new payment method in the Payment section of the app menu if your payment method was declined for one of the reasons below:

  • Card number entered is incorrect
  • Credit or debit card has expired
  • Insufficient funds in the account

You may need to contact your bank in the following situations:

  • You’re traveling abroad and haven’t authorized international transactions
  • The bank rejected the charge based on fraud
  • You’ve exceeded the withdrawal limit on your account
  • You’ve reported your card as lost or stolen

I have a disability. How do I request a wait time fee refund or waiver?

Riders with a disability can request a refund or waiver of wait time fees if their disability impacts their ability to board a vehicle within a few minutes of the driver’s arrival at the designated pickup location (currently 2 minutes for UberX or XL or 5 minutes for Black or Black SUV trips).

Wait Time Fee Refunds for Riders with Disabilities

You may request a refund if you have a disability (or traveled with a companion with a disability, or requested a ride on behalf a rider with a disability) and, in the last 30 days, you were charged wait time fees when you were at the pickup location when the driver arrived and needed more than the allotted time to board the vehicle due to disability.

Future Wait Time Fee Waivers for Riders with Disabilities

If you, or an individual that frequently accompanies you on trips, is (i) disabled within the meaning of the Americans with Disabilities Act, and (ii) the disability impacts your or your companion’s ability to board a vehicle within the allotted time after meeting the vehicle at your designated pickup location, you can request a waiver of wait time fees for your account below by making the following certification. Please allow at least 7 days for the waiver to take effect. If you are charged a wait time fee after you certify, please request a refund at the above link.

Certification of Disability

By entering my initials below and selecting Submit below, I voluntarily certify and acknowledge that:

I am, or an individual that frequently accompanies me on trips is, (i) disabled within the meaning of the Americans with Disabilities Act, and (ii) the disability impacts my or my companion’s ability to board a vehicle within the allotted time after meeting the vehicle at my designated pickup location. I understand that this request is subject to verification and/or audit by Uber. I further understand that Uber will use my certification for no other purpose than to waive wait time fees for my account. I also understand that Uber will not assume any additional duties or obligations in connection with this information or my certification.

As part of this process, Uber will ask you to verify your identity using a temporary verification code. This may require you to have a phone number attached to your account. If you’re not able to add a phone number in your account settings, contact us though the link below.

You won’t be able to delete your Uber account if you have an outstanding payment. Please clear the payment before deleting your account.

Once you delete your account, it will be immediately deactivated. After 30 days it will be permanently deleted, and any unused credits, promotions, or rewards will be removed.

Uber may retain certain information after account deletion as required or permitted by law.

Impacted Marketplace Surcharge + Seasonal Driver Surcharge

What is the Impacted Marketplace Surcharge?

Due to marketplace conditions, there is an additional fee on your trip. This surcharge is paid to drivers in full for rides during inclement weather or other marketplace disruptions.

What is the Seasonal Driver Surcharge?

Due to the increased demand for trips in ski town markets during winter months and beach/lake town/markets during summer months, an additional surcharge is applied to these trips. This surcharge is paid to drivers in full to help increase driver availability during this peak season to meet rider demand.

What is the UberMilitary Surcharge?

The UberMilitary rider-to-driver surcharge is used to improve reliability for trips that start & end on military bases. A small pool of eligible drivers with base access can complete these trips. This surcharge is paid to drivers in full to help increase driver availability to meet rider demand on military bases.

How will I know if my trip has a surcharge included?

When you request a trip, any applicable surcharge(s) will be automatically included in the upfront price or estimated prices range you see before requesting. The surcharge(s) will appear as separately itemized as part of the Tolls, Surcharges, and Fees item on your trip receipt.

To view your fare breakdown:

  1. Tap the price displayed next to the vehicle option.
  2. Tap the information icon.

The surcharge(s) will either appear as part of the Tolls, Surcharges, and Fees item on your trip receipt or will be separately itemized.

Items prohibited from package delivery

Items sent via Uber Connect must comply with all laws and regulations and with Uber’s policies. Sending illegal, unsafe, or other prohibited items listed on this page is strictly prohibited.

If you send an item in violation of the law or any of our policies, including those listed on this page, we will take corrective actions, as appropriate, including but not limited to, immediately suspending or terminating your account. Sending illegal or unsafe products can also lead to legal action, including civil and criminal penalties.

Uber encourages you to report items that violate Uber’s policies or applicable law by contacting us. We will investigate each report thoroughly and take appropriate action.

The following items are prohibited from package delivery:

  • Illegal items;
  • Firearms, weaponry, and their parts
  • Alcohol
  • Highly perishable food or beverages (e.g., raw meat or dairy products), pharmaceutical products, over-the-counter medications, vitamins or supplements;
  • Recreational drugs, drug paraphernalia, or tobacco products;
  • Money, giftcards, lottery tickets or transferable securities;
  • Dangerous or hazardous items, including explosives, items that are poisonous or flammable, medical waste, items that are dangerous for other items, equipment, transportation vehicles or health and safety of people and other dangerous or hazardous items as described in the applicable regulations;
  • Stolen goods and;
  • Fragile items;
  • live animals

The above list of prohibited items is not exhaustive. Uber retains the discretion to prohibit additional items that are not on the above list.

Joining my Company’s Uber for Business Account

What is Uber for Business?

Uber for Business simplifies expense management for companies, offering employees free or subsidized rides, Uber Eats orders, and other perks beyond regular business travel.

How to get started

To use Uber for Business, link your existing Uber account (likely tied to your personal email) to your company’s profile. Don’t create a new account—using your personal account lets you manage both personal and business profiles with a single login.

Once logged into your personal Uber account, accept your Uber for Business invitation to create a business profile.

Linking your account

From your web browser:
  1. Open the invitation email sent to your work email
  2. Click the link in the email
  3. Sign in using your personal Uber account email
  4. Follow the prompts to finalize setup

If you encounter technical issues from a web browser: * Use Incognito mode or a different browser * Clear your browser’s cache and cookies

From your app:
  1. Download and update the Uber app
  2. Open the app and log in using your personal account credentials

If you encounter technical issues with the app: * Close and reopen the app * If the issue persists, uninstall and reinstall the app

3 ways to link your account in the app:
1. Accepting the invitation
  1. Open the invitation email on your device
  2. Tap the Or, sign in and create an account here link, which will open the Uber app
  3. Sign in using your personal credentials
2. Using the QR code
  1. Open the invitation email on another device
  2. Scan the QR code using your phone’s camera, which will open the app
  3. Tap Join now and follow the steps
3. Using a PIN code
  1. Open the invitation email on your device
  2. Copy the PIN code
  3. In the Uber app, go to Account
  4. Select Wallet
  5. Select I have a code, enter your PIN, and follow the steps

Troubleshooting common issues

Can’t find your invitation email?

  1. Check your junk or spam folder. The email will come from Uber for Business (noreply@uber.com) and the subject will include your company name.

  2. Request a new invitation here by logging in and entering your email address.

  3. Still no email? Contact Uber for Business Support at business-support@uber.com.

    Seeing “Email already in use” or “Phone number already in use”?

  • This happens if you’re trying to create a new account instead of logging into your personal one. Simply log in with your existing credentials when prompted.

  • Still having issues? Contact Uber for Business Support at business-support@uber.com.

    Received “Pending approval” after accepting the invitation?

  • Your request is under review by your company administrator. Once approved, your accounts will link automatically, and you’ll be able to access your benefits right away.

    Have you added a valid payment method?

    Even if your company covers all costs, a personal payment method is required to verify your account. Confirm you’ve added one to complete the linking process.

    QR code or PIN code not working?

  • Use only your personal invitation email—forwarded emails or someone else’s PIN won’t work.

  • Invitations expire after 365 days. If expired, contact Uber support or:

  1. Open the Uber app
  2. Go to Account > Business Hub
  3. Select Accept your business invite/set up your business profile
  4. Select Get Started, then Next to resend the invite to your work email

Need more help?

Check out this video or reach out to your business administrator or Uber Support for more assistance.

If you’re unsure who your administrator is, check with your HR department, help desk, or Uber Support.

My driver made inappropriate comments about my service animal

State and federal law prohibit drivers who are using the Uber Driver App from denying service to riders with service animals because of the service animals, and from otherwise discriminating against riders with service animals.

Drivers who use the Uber apps agree to follow our community guidelines, which includes being respectful of riders traveling with a service animal.

My driver refused my destination

If your driver refused your destination, let us know what happened below.

It is a good practice to contact your driver if you’re planning to travel a long distance. Many drivers need to make the return trip home after reaching your destination, so it’s helpful to let your driver know it’ll be a long trip right after they accept your request.

Note: On some trip request types, riders may be asked to walk a short way during the pickup and/or drop-off process.

If you believe you were incorrectly charged a cancellation fee, go back in your trip history and select the trip in question. Under the trip, select Review my cancellation fee and we’ll look into it.

My driver was rude or unprofessional

Drivers who drive with Uber agree to maintain a high standard of professional service that includes being respectful and polite. If that was not your experience, please share the details with us on this page.

You can help us improve trip safety and quality by providing a rating for your driver. If you haven’t rated your driver yet, select the trip from the Trips section of the menu in your Uber app to provide a rating.

If you believe you were unfairly charged a cancellation fee, please report using this link.

If your driver refused your destination, let us know what happened below.

If you believe you were incorrectly charged a cancellation fee, go back in your trip history and select the trip in question. Under the trip, select Review my cancellation fee and we’ll look into it.

If your driver made discriminatory remarks towards you, please use the link below to report it as a safety issue. We take reports of discrimination very seriously.

If your driver’s behavior made you feel unsafe, please report using the link below:
