Can we help with anything else?

Event or venue surcharges

Additional surcharges may apply to your trip in certain cities or certain parts of a city. Certain venues or events may have pickup and dropoff zones that extend up to a few blocks away from the property. In these cases, surcharges may overlap with neighboring properties and affect trips within the area.

Depending on the charge and location, these additional charges may be retained by your driver, or paid by your driver to Uber or Uber-related entities. In some cases, these charges are then remitted to third-party entities, such as partner companies, or event and venue operators.

If you’re viewing this on the web at help.uber.com, make sure to select your city at the bottom right of the page to get the most accurate information for your region.

Surcharge types and uses

Surcharges are collected for several different purposes depending on the event and/or venue in question, and help ensure a more intuitive, reliable, and comfortable experience for both drivers and riders.

Driver event surcharge

These surcharges:

  • Apply to trips beginning or ending at large event locations that are expected to generate a lot of requests and complex dropoffs and pickups, which often present logistical challenges at the event location
  • Are paid partially or in full to drivers to help increase drivers for the event through earnings promotions
Uber venue or event surcharge

These surcharges:

  • Apply to trips beginning or ending at or near certain venues or special events where an elevated pickup and dropoff experience has been put in place for riders and drivers
  • Are collected to offset costs that Uber and/or the event or venue operator incur to help improve the experience and can include designated seating areas, wifi access, phone charging facilities, tenting, and instructional signage for riders, as well as in-app instructions and manual pickup and dropoff zone refinement, staffing, wayfinding and/or traffic infrastructure for a smoother pickup and dropoff
    • In some locations, the venue or event surcharges may be added to the driver’s earnings — either partially or fully — to increase earning opportunities and to help ensure a more reliable experience. These surcharges may be displayed as incentives on the driver’s earnings statement.

How to know if a trip has a surcharge

When you request a trip, the event surcharges will be automatically included in the upfront price or estimated price range you see before requesting. You can also view the amount of the surcharges on the Fare Breakdown screen within the app.

To get to the fare breakdown:

  1. Select the price displayed next to the vehicle option
  2. Select the information icon

The surcharges will either appear as part of the Tolls, Surcharges, and Fees item on your trip receipt or will be separately itemized.

How is the price of a trip determined?

In most cities, riders are offered an upfront price. Many data points go into calculating an upfront price, including the estimated trip time, distance from origin to destination, time of day, route, and demand patterns. It also includes tolls, taxes, surcharges, and fees (with the exception of wait time fees).

The upfront price you’re shown may change due to a number of circumstances, which may include adding stops, updating your destination, significant changes to the route or duration of the trip, or you pass through a toll that was not factored into your upfront price. In addition, you may incur wait time fees for the time you take to get to the car at the pickup or multi-stop fees for time spent at an on-trip stop.

Additionally, under certain circumstances, we may adjust your trip payment after a trip–for example, a driver notifies us that he or she paid the cash rate at a toll rather than the toll pass rate (which we use as the default reimbursement rate from the rider to the driver for all tolls).

If an upfront price is not honored, you will either be charged the minimum price or a price based on the measured time and distance for your trip, including any base rate, booking fee, surcharges, tolls, and other relevant factors such as a dynamic pricing charge.

You’ll always get a receipt for any trip price. If your final charge is different than the upfront price you agreed to, your receipt will explain why.

Please note that, as of August 10, 2022, multi-stop fees are not live in all US markets.

Post-trip prices

Some cities do not provide upfront prices. Instead, you’re charged either a minimum price or a price based on the time and distance for your trip’s route, which may include a base fare, a Booking Fee, surcharges, tolls, and dynamic pricing. Prices may vary by location, the vehicle option you select, and other factors.

Industrial Funding Fee (IFF)

Each ride taken by a government employee using their Uber for Business GSA Profile will be charged an Industrial Funding Fee (IFF) that is entirely passed on to the General Services Administration (GSA). The IFF charged is based on the following fee components:

  • 2% of the value of the ride/transaction (excluding any additional tolls, taxes, fees, or tips)
  • The full amount of the IFF will be sent to the GSA, as mandated by contractual terms, and will not be paid to the driver or to Uber.

Long Pickup Premiums

Long Pickup Premiums are charged for trips where drivers must travel a longer distance or time to reach the pickup location. This amount is estimated and included in your upfront price.

The quoted upfront price may change due to circumstances such as adding stops, updating your destination, significant changes to the route or trip duration, or passing through tolls not factored into your upfront price. Extra fees may apply for extended wait times at the pickup location or time spent at stops on multi-stop trips. Riders can view applicable rates, including the Long Pickup Premium, by selecting the upfront price, then Information in the app.

Time and distance pricing does not apply to Uber Reserve, which is paid as an upfront fare.

As of August 10, 2022, multi-stop fees are not available in all US markets.

Other charges and fees

You may incur additional fees in these situations:

Tolls and surcharges

These charges may apply when:

  • Tolls are expected on your route
  • Governmental fees are imposed on rideshare services in the area where you’re requesting your trip
  • You request trips to or from airports, seaports, stadiums, and other venues/events

If the tolls or surcharges on your route differ from our predicted route, the price of your ride may change.

Depending on the charge and location, these additional charges may either be:

  • Kept by your driver
  • Paid by your driver to Uber or related entities
  • Passed through (in whole or in part) to a governmental agency or other third parties

Tolls or surcharges included in your trip price may not match the amount retained by the parties mentioned above. Also, they may not correspond to any third-party or government charge; and may relate to charges or inconveniences occuring before or after your trip.

You can learn more about these fees on our Tolls and surcharges and Airport trips help pages.

Car Seat trips

Uber Car Seat rides in NYC and LA include a Nuna RAVA seat that can be used in rear-facing or forward-facing positions for children weighing between 5 and 65 pounds. A $10 surcharge is added to UberX pricing for Uber Car Seat trips.

You can learn more from our Uber Car Seat help page.

Excessive luggage and/or passengers

If you’re traveling with several other riders or pieces of luggage, you can request a larger vehicle option to help avoid unexpected charges or pickup issues.

In many cities, Uber offers larger vehicle options like UberXL and UberSUV. These vehicle options have the space to accommodate 6 or more riders.

Can we help with anything else?

Tolls and surcharges

When tolls and surcharges may apply

If the predicted route used in your upfront price includes an estimated toll, surcharge or other fee, it’s automatically included in your trip price.These charges can include (but are not limited to):

  • Tolls associated with your trip’s route
  • Tolls associated with your driver’s return to the area where they usually conduct trips. If your driver must pay a toll to return after your trip ends, that return toll may be included in your upfront price so the driver isn’t left covering the cost.
  • Surcharges to or from airports, seaports, stadiums and other venues & events
  • City-specific surcharges meant to offset governmental fees imposed on rideshare services
  • A Temporary Fuel Surcharge, which is passed directly from the rider to the driver

If the tolls or surcharges on your route are different from your predicted route, the price of your ride may change.

If your driver chooses to take an optional express or managed lane during your trip, that toll will not be added to your upfront price, as optional express lane tolls are not included in trip prices or reimbursed to drivers.

Who keeps the tolls and surcharges

Depending on the reason and location, these additional charges may either be:

  • Kept by your driver
  • Paid by your driver to Uber or related entities
  • Passed through (in whole or in part) to a governmental agency or other third parties

The charged amount may not match the amount kept by the parties mentioned above. Here are some examples:

  • You may be charged an estimated toll at the commercial toll rate even though your driver pays the non-commercial rate
  • You may be charged an estimated toll for your driver’s return to the area in which they usually conduct trips, even if the toll doesn’t apply in the direction of your trip’s route
  • The tolls or surcharges you’re charged may differ from the actual amount paid by a driver, such as when electronic and cash toll amounts differ
  • In certain areas, a surcharge is added to your trip price even though the location doesn’t collect a corresponding payment from Uber or your driver
  • Depending on the area and the charge(s), passengers on an UberX Share trip may each pay the full amount of the estimated charge(s)
  • You may be charged an estimated amount for dynamic tolls. Because Uber cannot calculate live toll prices in real time, these estimates are based on historical averages and may differ from the actual amount paid by your driver.

Uber Military Driver Surcharge

What is the Uber Military surcharge?

The Uber Military surcharge is a fee added to trips that start and end on military bases. This surcharge helps improve reliability by supporting a small group of eligible drivers who have access to these locations. Drivers receive 100% of this surcharge to help increase driver availability and meet rider requests on military bases.

How is the surcharge shown in my trip price?

Any applicable surcharge will be automatically included in the upfront price or estimated price range before you request a ride. You will see the surcharge itemized on your trip receipt under Tolls, Surcharges, and Fees.

Who receives the Uber Military surcharge?

The full amount of the Uber Military surcharge is paid directly to the driver who completes your trip on a military base.

Where can I find more details about my trip charges?

You can see a breakdown of your trip charges, including any surcharges, on your trip receipt in the app. For more information about how pricing works, see How are Fares Calculated page.

Why is this surcharge important?

This surcharge helps ensure there are enough drivers available to serve riders on military bases by helping to cover the extra requirements for base access. It helps keep wait times low and improves reliability for riders at these locations.

Can we help with anything else?

What is the WA Driver Resource Center Fund Fee?

The WA Driver Resource Center Fund Fee, implemented on July 1, 2024, as a result of the passing of Washington’s House Bill 2076, is a mandatory fee that is applied to all trips that originate in Washington completed by ridesharing drivers. The funds generated from the fee go towards the Washington Driver Resource Center to support the driver community.

Promos and partnerships

American Express® Gold Card Benefit

You can receive up to $120 in Uber Cash annually by adding your U.S. Consumer American Express® Gold Card (“Amex Card”) as a payment method to your Uber account.

  • Uber Cash received through Uber with your Amex Card is distributed in monthly increments of $10 and can be used for rides or orders in the U.S. when you select an Amex Card for your transaction (the “Amex Benefit”).
  • The Amex Benefit will expire at the end of each month and won’t roll over to the following month.

When your Amex Benefit isn’t enough to cover the cost of your ride or order, the Amex Card in your Uber wallet selected for the transaction will be charged the difference. If Uber is unable to use the Amex Card in your Uber wallet to complete the transaction, the Amex Benefit will not be applied and another available payment method in your Uber wallet will be charged for the transaction. The Amex Benefit will not automatically apply to rides or orders when using a business profile. To use the Amex Benefit, you must toggle Uber Cash on in your business profile, which may be restricted based on your employer organization’s settings for such profile.

Follow these steps to redeem the Amex Benefit when requesting a ride or placing an order:

  1. Enter your destination in the Uber app or create an order in the Uber Eats app or Postmates App
  2. At checkout, select an American Express Card as the payment method for your ride or order and make sure Uber Cash is toggled on
  3. Request your ride or place your order

To find your Uber Cash balance

  1. Select Account in the bottom right corner of your app
  2. Select Wallet/Payment
  3. From there, you can also view your Amex Benefit history by tapping Uber balances

You’ll receive the Amex Benefit each month as long as your Amex Card remains eligible and is a payment method on your Uber account. If your eligible Amex Card is added to multiple Uber accounts, the account to which you first added the Amex Card will receive the associated Amex Benefit.

Adding an eligible Card to your Uber account

  1. Go to the Wallet/Payment section of the Uber, Uber Eats, and/or Postmates apps
  2. Select Add Payment Method and enter your eligible Amex Card information

Basic Card Members with the Amex Card are eligible. You’ll receive $10 in Uber Cash each month for each eligible Card on your Uber account.

If you do not see this Uber Cash in your Uber Cash balance within 48 hours from the time you enroll, call the number on the back of your Card for assistance.

Switching which Uber account receives the Uber Cash Amex Benefit

  1. Delete the Amex Card from all Uber accounts that you no longer wish to receive the Amex Benefit
  2. Add the Card as a payment method on the Uber account where you do wish to receive the Amex Benefit. If the Amex Card is already on this account, skip this step

The Amex Benefit will switch accounts and apply to that new account starting on the 1st day of the next month. You can always re-add your eligible Amex Card to other Uber accounts after completing these steps if you want the users to be able to pay for Uber rides and orders with the Card.

If you have specific questions about your American Express Card or other American Express benefits, please call the number on the back of your American Express Card.

For more information and to see the Terms and Conditions, please visit uber.com/amex.

If you have additional questions about this benefit, contact us below.

Connecting Life360 with Uber

What is Life360?

Life360’s mission is to enhance family safety and connection by providing a comprehensive platform for location sharing, communication, and security.

How do I link my Uber and Life360 accounts?

Open the Life360 app and tap on any Family or Teen-related entry point. You’ll be prompted to connect your Uber account in just a few steps.

Do I need to already have an Uber account?

You will need an Uber account! If you don’t already have an Uber account, you can create one as part of the linking process.

How do I see my teen’s rides in Life360?

A car icon will appear next to their profile on the Life360 map, along with their estimated arrival time. The full ride tracking experience, including real-time updates and access to safety features, are available within the Uber app.

Do I need a Life360 subscription to use this?

You need an active Life360 account, no paying subscription required.

Who is eligible for the Life360 Gold 6 month free-trial offer?

Any Uber One member who is within a linked Circle and not a current paying Life360 member is eligible to redeem the Life360 Gold 6-month free trial. Terms apply.

Is this available everywhere?

Currently, you can only link your Life360 account to your Uber account in the United States (excluding California). Uber teen accounts are available in select cities.

Can I request a ride for myself or my teen from the Life360 app?

Yes! As a Family Organizer, you can request a ride from Life360. It will open the Uber app so you can get your teen (age 13-17) where they need to go. Your teen will need to have an Uber teen account set up. Learn more about how to get their teen account started here. Users must have an active Uber account to use.

What happens if I unlink my account?

You can unlink your Uber and Life360 Circles at any time within the Life360 app.

If you have redeemed either the Uber One free trial or Life360 gold free trial, unlinking will not terminate this membership. You can cancel your Uber One membership in the Uber app and your Life360 membership in the Life360 app.

Where can I get extra help?

Life360 Issues?

For help with your Life360 account, Circle, or similar questions related to your experience with Life360, you can contact Life360 support by visiting their Help Center.

Uber Issues?

If you have questions about account linking, support for Uber rides or Uber Eats orders, or other questions related to your experience with Uber, please contact us below.

Earn miles with Delta on Uber

Connecting your Uber account to your SkyMiles® account allows you to earn on eligible Uber rides and Uber Eats orders.

Link your Uber account to your Delta SkyMiles® account

You must have a Delta SkyMiles® account. If you’re not a Delta SkyMiles® member, you can sign up for free here. When you’re ready to start earning, you can link your account from the Uber app. To do this:

  1. Tap Account in the bottom right corner of the Uber app.
  2. Scroll to the bottom of the page and choose Partner Rewards.
  3. Tap Delta SkyMiles®.
  4. Tap Link Account on the next screen and login to your Delta SkyMiles® account to successfully link accounts.

How to earn Delta Sky Miles

Once you’ve linked your account and selected “Delta SkyMiles®” as your Earning Partner, you just need to make a Qualifying Transaction according to the Delta Program Terms.

Do I need to link my Delta SkyMiles® account in both the Uber Eats and Uber apps to earn points?

You will only need to link your Delta SkyMiles account with Uber once. When you link through either the Uber Eats or Uber app, you’ll be ready to start earning across both rides and Uber Eats. You can only earn points or miles with one loyalty partner at a time; if you are earning with another partner and would like to switch your active Earning Partner to Delta, navigate to the Settings section of your app and then, under Partner Rewards, check to confirm.

What can I use miles with Delta on?

You can use your miles toward travel to 1,000+ destinations, a Delta Vacations® package, cars and hotels booked through Delta, or for day of travel perks like seat upgrades and checked bags. Terms and conditions apply. Learn more about how to use miles with Delta. the Delta SkyMiles Program here.

Do all Uber rides qualify to earn miles?

No, not all rides are eligible to earn. While you can earn miles on qualifying rides and orders, including restaurant and grocery orders, UberX trips to the airport, Premium Rides, and rides booked ahead of time with Uber Reserve, there are some rides that are not eligible. These include trips taken on Family Profiles or Teen accounts, along with rides booked through the Guest Rides feature. Other terms apply. Please review the full Delta Program Terms here.

Can I earn both Marriott Bonvoy points and miles with Delta on my rides and orders?

While you are able to link to both programs, you will need to choose which partner you want to earn with by making them your Earning Partner in the App. You can only earn points or miles with one loyalty partner at a time.

How does earning work with split fares?

You will only earn on the portion of the Qualifying Transaction (as defined in the Delta & Uber program terms) that is actually attributed (i.e. charged) to your Uber account. The earn status of other riders will only be considered for the portion of the Qualifying Transaction that is actually attributed to their respective Uber account(s). For example, if you split a fare with a friend, each of you will earn only on the portion of the fare you paid for, respectively, not the total ride amount.

If you have questions about account linking, support for trips or orders, or similar questions related to your experience with Uber, please contact us below.

Earning Points with Aeroplan

Aeroplan has partnered with Uber allowing Aeroplan members to link their Aeroplan account through the Uber or Uber Eats apps. Linked members can earn Aeroplan points on both eligible rides with Uber and eligible Uber Eats orders. If you aren’t an Aeroplan Member, you can join in less than 60 seconds for free at Aeroplan.com. Only one Aeroplan Member account can be linked per Uber user.

You can earn:

  • Points and flight credits on eligible Uber rides in Canada
  • Aeroplan points on eligible Uber Eats orders when they’re placed in Canada

Air Canada flight credit

After you’ve linked your Aeroplan and Uber accounts, you’ll earn an Air Canada flight credit for every 10 Uber rides you take (excluding UberX Share) in Canada. The flight credit will appear in your Aeroplan Member dashboard within 6-8 weeks.

Linked members can use their Air Canada flight credit for any new Air Canada, Air Canada Express, and Air Canada Rouge booking made directly through an Air Canada channel such as aircanada.com or the Air Canada mobile app. The Air Canada flight credit can’t be used for Air Canada Vacations or for any other airline (for example, United Airlines, Lufthansa) booking even if made through Air Canada.

Extending expired promotions

Promo codes:

  • Won’t work after the expiration date
  • Can’t be extended or renewed

To view which promotions are currently applied to your account:

  1. Tap “Account.”
  2. Tap “Wallet.”
  3. Tap “Promotions.” You’ll see any current and expired promotions listed.
  4. Tap a promo to view its details.

Promotions FAQs

What are promo codes, and how do they work?

When using a promo code, please keep the following in mind:

  • Promos must be applied before requesting a trip. They can’t be applied after placing an order.
  • Promotions will automatically be applied first. We select the promotion that has the higher maximum discount and automatically apply it to your next ride.
  • If your promo was a percentage off, this means we compared the maximum discount listed in the terms and conditions to determine the best savings for your ride.

How do I add a promo code to my account?

  1. Tap the profile icon at the bottom of the main screen
  2. Select Wallet
  3. Select Add Promo Code
  4. Enter the code and select Add

How do I view active promotions?

  1. Tap the profile icon at the bottom of the main screen
  2. Select Wallet
  3. Select Promotions

Active promotions appear at the top of the page and display the expiration date and country within which they can be used.

How do I add multiple promotions on a trip?

You can add as many promo codes to your account as you’d like, but only one will apply per trip taken. The code with the highest discount will be applied, so if you’re holding on to a particular code for a specific trip you should enter it right before you take that trip. Any difference between the promo and fare amount will be charged to your payment method selected, and any value that isn’t used will not roll over.

Many promo codes are also reserved for new users only, so you can apply a maximum of one new user code to your account before your first trip.

Applying promotions across currencies

Promotions can only be redeemed in the currency in which they were initially issued. Any credits/Uber Cash for other currencies/areas will remain on your account and automatically apply to your next eligible ride.

How do I get promotions?

Most of the promotional offers you receive are unique to your profile, which means that you may receive a promotion other users in your area may not have access to while they receive other promotional offers.

The best way to keep up with our promotions is to watch your email for a message from us.

How do I invite friends using my promo code?

To invite a friend to use Uber and receive a discount on their first ride:

  1. Tap the menu icon in the top left corner of your Uber app
  2. Select Free Rides
  3. Select the send icon to text or email your invite code to a friend or post it to social media, or tap Send Invites

Your invitees will receive a discount on their first ride when they use your invite code. Invite codes are valid only for new users. If you are unable to view these options under the Account tab of your app, it is possible that access to this promotion isn’t available to you at this time.

Still having trouble? Fill out the form below and we’ll be in touch to help.

Uber Loyalty Partner Programs

Currently, Uber offers partner loyalty linking with Marriott and Delta Air Lines.

How to Earn Rewards with a Partner

To begin earning rewards with a partner, you must:

  1. Link your Uber account to the partner account in your Uber App.
  2. Select the partner as your Earning Partner in your Uber app.
  3. Make a Qualifying Transaction according to the applicable partner’s Offer Terms.

Can I earn both Marriott Bonvoy points and miles with Delta on my rides and orders?

While you are able to link to both programs, you will need to choose which partner you want to earn with by making them your Earning Partner in the App. You can only earn points or miles with one loyalty partner at a time.

How do I choose which partner to earn rewards with?

After linking your accounts, you’ll need to select a single Earning Partner in the Uber app. Once selected, you’ll earn rewards with that partner on all eligible rides and Uber Eats orders, according to their respective offer terms.

If you’d like to switch partners, you can do so in the app—but it may take up to an hour for changes to take effect, and you won’t earn retroactively for past trips or orders.

Each partner offers different earn structures depending on the ride type or order. For example:

  • With Delta, you can earn up to 3 miles per eligible dollar on all Uber Reserve trips and 1–2 miles per eligible dollar on eligible airport and premium rides.
  • With Marriott Bonvoy, you can earn up to 3 points per dollar on Premium rides (e.g., Uber XL, Comfort, Black) and 2x points on Uber Eats orders over $40 delivered to select Marriott properties (like Residence Inn® and Element®).

Please note that additional terms and conditions apply to each offer, and we recommend reviewing each partner’s full earn terms before choosing your Earning Partner.

Viewing your Earning Partner

To view your active earning partner:

  1. Navigate to the Settings section of your app.
  2. Under Partner Rewards, check to confirm.

How do I switch my earning partner?

You’ll need to have your earning partner linked before your trip is completed. It can take up to one hour to switch earning partners.

Can I retroactively earn on qualifying trips or orders?

No. You must be linked and have a partner selected as your “Earning Partner” when your trip is completed.

Learn more about Partner Loyalty Programs

  • To view the Delta Program Details, see here.
  • To view the Marriott Program Details, see here.

What is Signature Support for Amex?

Signature Support is a dedicated, 24/7 phone support benefit available to eligible U.S. Consumer American Express Platinum Card® and Centurion® Members on Uber rides, Uber Eats orders, and Postmates orders.

Who is eligible for Signature Support?

You’re eligible for Signature Support if you:

  • Are a U.S. Consumer American Express Platinum Card® or Centurion® Member
  • Have added your eligible American Express Card to your Uber Wallet, and
  • Are using Uber, Uber Eats, or Postmates on an iOS or Android device

If an eligible American Express Card is added to multiple Uber accounts, Signature Support will apply only to the first Uber account to which the Card was added.

How to access Signature Support?

Eligible users can access Signature Support through two in-app entry points.

  • Partner Rewards Hub: You can find Signature Support by navigating to the Partner Rewards Hub in the Uber, Uber Eats, or Postmates app. To reach the Partner Rewards hub:

    1. Open the Uber, Uber Eats, or Postmates app
    2. Select Account
    3. Select Partner Rewards

  • Help Section: You can also access Signature Support within the Help section of the apps, by selecting a help topic for Rides or Eats and navigating to “Call Signature Support.” To reach the Help section, select “Account” and then “Help.”

Note: Eligibility and access may take up to 24 hours to update after adding an eligible American Express Card to your Uber Wallet.

Using Signature Support phone assistance

  • The dedicated phone number is displayed only in the Partner Rewards Hub or within the Help section in the apps
  • Calls must be placed from the phone number associated with your Uber account
  • If your account is not eligible, the phone support option may not appear, or you may not be able to connect with Uber Support.

Adding an eligible card to your Uber account

To add an eligible American Express Card:

  1. Open the Uber, Uber Eats, or Postmates app
  2. Select Account
  3. Go to Wallet / Payment
  4. Select Add Payment Method
  5. Add your eligible U.S. Consumer American Express Platinum Card® or Centurion® Card

If your eligible Card is already added, no further action is required.

For more information and to see the full Terms and Conditions, please visit: uber.com/amex

If you have questions about your American Express Card benefits, please call the number on the back of your Card.

If you have additional questions about Signature Support, contact us below.

FAQs about invoicing

Make sure your tax profile is correctly registered before reporting any problem with your billing. If you have questions about your tax profile, please refer to the following article:

Frequently asked questions about my tax profile

How can I request my invoices?

  1. Go to the trips section in your profile.
  2. Locate and select the trip.
  3. Click on “view details”.
  4. In the upper right corner, select the invoice request option.

After requesting your invoice, it will be sent shortly to the email associated with your Uber account.

If you need to download your invoice again, you can follow the same steps.

How does automatic billing work?

By activating automatic billing, each of your trips will be invoiced once completed. You will receive your invoices in the email associated with your Uber account.

To activate automatic billing, go to your tax profile and in the second to last field, select “Automatic billing”.

How long do I have to issue my invoice?

Trips made in Mexico that have been successfully paid will be available for immediate invoicing at riders.uber.com/trips.

To request an invoice or a change in support, you will have up to 90 calendar days after the trip was made.

How to get a trip receipt

When a trip ends, we automatically send a receipt to the email address on your Uber account. If you aren’t receiving receipts, check the spam or junk folders of this email address. You can review your email address by selecting “Settings” from your app menu. Tap your email address to update it.

Your Uber account includes a trip history of every ride you’ve taken. Below are several ways you can view your receipts:

In-App: 1. Open the Uber app menu and tap “Your Trips.” 2. Select the desired trip. 3. On the right side of the screen, underneath the amount paid, tap “Receipt.”

Web: 1. Login to 0. 2. Click on “My Trips” and select the desired trip. 3. Click on “View Receipt” or “Resend Receipt.” If you clicked on “View Receipt,” you’ll be able to print, resend, or download it as a PDF from there.

Help.uber.com: 1. Navigate to help.uber.com and log in to your Uber account. 2. When you see the information for your most recent trip request, use the drop-down menu to select a specific trip. 3. Once the appropriate trip is selected, click on “Receipt” to view.

Downloading a PDF via Email: 1. At the bottom of your emailed receipt in the left-hand corner, click on “Download PDF.”

I’m not receiving any emails or receipts

If you aren’t receiving ride receipts or emails from Uber, first check your spam or junk folders. Make sure to mark emails from Uber as “not junk” so you receive them in the future.

If you can’t find Uber emails, try updating your email in the app:

  1. Open your Uber app menu and tap “Settings”
  2. Tap your name at the top of the menu
  3. Select your email to update it (if necessary)
  4. Tap “Save”

You can also view trip receipts in the app or on the web:

In-app:

  1. Open the Uber App menu and tap “Your Trips.”
  2. Select the desired trip.
  3. On the right side of your screen, underneath the amount paid, tap “Receipt.”

Web:

  1. Login to 0.
  2. Click on “My Trips” and select the desired trip.
  3. Click on “View Receipt” or “Resend Receipt.”
  4. If you clicked on “View Receipt,” from there you”ll be able to print your receipt, download it as a PDF or resend it by email.

help.uber.com:

  1. Navigate to help.uber.com and log in to your Uber account.
  2. When you see the information for your most recent trip request, use the drop-down menu to select a specific trip.
  3. Once the appropriate trip is selected, click on “Receipt” to view.

Downloading a PDF through email:

At the bottom of your emailed receipt in the left-hand corner, click on “Download PDF.”

Tax Invoices: Bulk Data Export on external portals

Looking to download multiple tax invoices at once? You can now export your invoices in bulk directly from your portal.

Uber is launching the Bulk Data Export functionality for Tax Invoices on external portals. This initiative extends the already live internal Bulk Export capability.

This includes:

  • Drivers (Driver Web Portal)
  • Delivery Partners (Driver Web Portal)
  • Merchants (Uber Eats Manager)

What does this new functionality offer users?

  • Export invoices in bulk.
  • Filter invoices by:
    • Timeframe
    • Invoice type (invoice edge)
    • Driver / Delivery partner / Merchant
  • Choose delivery method:
    • Direct download
    • Email (up to 5 recipients)
  • Select export format:
    • PDF
    • XML
    • JWS (Poland)
  • Monitor export status (Processing / Completed / Failed).
  • Access exports for up to 7 days.

How the New Help Page (External Portals) works

  1. Go to the Invoices or Trip Invoices section of your portal.
  2. Use filters (timeframe, invoice type, etc.) to refine the results.
  3. Select “Export multiple invoices”.
  4. Choose:
    • File format (PDF, XML, CSV where available)
    • Delivery method (Download or Email)
  5. Submit your request.

Next steps after requesting your invoices:

  • You’ll see the request status in the Export Requests tab.
  • You’ll receive an email when the export is ready (if email option selected).
  • Files are available for download for 7 days.

Please consider the following:

  • Only successfully generated invoices will be exported.
  • Invoices with errors will not be included.
  • If your export fails, you’ll see the reason and can try again or contact support.

Rentals by Uber

About Car Rentals through Uber

With Car Rentals through Uber, customers have the ability to book a rental car directly on our platform with options tailored directly to your needs, style, and budget. Renting a car through Uber lets you pick from a variety of cars, whether you’re looking for a compact car for city driving, a spacious SUV for a road trip, or a stylish ride for a special occasion. There’s a car for every need and every budget. We partner with over 1700 rental car providers and can provide you with options for rental through companies like Sixt, Hertz, and many more.

You have the option to pick up the vehicle on your own at the rental counter or, in select cities in the United States only, you may be able to use our Car Delivery service and have the vehicle delivered to you. If you select the Car Delivery option, we will assign a Car Delivery Driver to pick up and drop off the car at a location you specify, allowing you to skip the rental counter altogether!

For more detailed information on Car Rentals through Uber, please check our FAQ pages:

Rental FAQs

Supplier FAQs

Looking for a vehicle to drive and earn with Uber?

Uber Rent offers cars for personal use only and can’t be used to earn on the Uber platform.

If you’d like a vehicle for driving and earning, please visit the Uber Vehicle Marketplace in the app or online.

Still have questions on Car Rentals through Uber? Chat with us below!

I need help with a current rental booking

We’re happy to assist you with your current reservation. If you’re at the rental car counter and need immediate assistance, please chat with us below.

You can also review our rental FAQs and the following common issues for quicker resolution:

I’m trying to pick up my car, but the rental desk is closed/there is no one here to assist me

First, please try to call the rental company. You can find the phone number of your supplier in the FAQs on your reservation page or on the rental voucher.

If the rental company doesn’t answer the phone, please take a picture of the closed rental desk, with the date and time showing in the picture.

Please send us the following information in the chat so we can try to assist you:

  • The picture of the closed rental desk
  • A screenshot of the call log, showing that you attempted to call the rental desk
  • Any proof showing you arrived at the rental desk on time (Uber/Taxi receipt, flight information, public transport ticket, Google Maps location information, etc)

The supplier didn’t have a rental car available for my rental

Please ask the rental desk for a note, email, or letter confirming they do not have a car available, and send the following in the chat window with us:

  • The note, email, or letter confirming that there was no car available
  • Any proof showing you arrived at the rental desk on time (Uber/Taxi receipt, flight information, public transport ticket, Google Maps location information, etc)

The deposit was higher than I had anticipated

Please ask the rental desk for a document that shows that the amount is higher than advised in the booking, and send this to us over the chat window. The security deposit is a common practice in the car rental industry to protect both the car rental company and the renter. It serves as a form of insurance in case of any unforeseen events during the rental period, such as damage to the vehicle or non-compliance with rental conditions. The deposit helps cover possible repair costs, fuel expenses, or unpaid fines.

The exact amount of the deposit may vary depending on the car rental company and the type of car you choose, but it is usually around CAD 500 for small vehicles. Please note that some car rental companies usually only accept credit cards for the deposit, and some only accept Visa or Mastercard.

It is essential to review the “Terms and Conditions” before booking to make sure you have the right payment method. Rest assured that the deposit is refundable, as long as there are no problems at the end of the rental contract.

The vehicle I received is unsafe, has mechanical issues, or has a cleanliness concern

If there’s a major concern about the vehicle’s condition or you believe it’s not safe to drive, please work with the rental supplier directly. If the issue persists, please chat with us below.

I need to cancel or modify a reservation

For reservations that you’ve scheduled to pay at the rental counter, you can cancel at any time without incurring any cancellation fees.

If you have a Pay now & Save (pay in app) reservation that you need to modify or cancel, and it’s more than 48 hours before your rental pickup time, you’re able to make changes in the Uber app.

Please note: Android users are currently unable to modify reservations directly from their app. If you need to make changes and have an Android, you will need to cancel the reservation and rebook with your updated details.

To Modify Your Reservation

  • Select Manage Reservation for the rental you wish to modify
  • In the Reservation Time section, update your trip’s date and time to reflect your new preferences
  • In the Pickup & Dropoff section, adjust the selected locations as needed
  • Review and confirm your changes

To Cancel Your Reservation

  • Select Manage Reservation for the rental you wish to cancel
  • Choose Cancel Reservation to proceed with the cancellation
  • Follow the on-screen prompts to complete the cancellation process

Please note that a cancellation fee may apply. Be sure to review the Terms and Conditions for additional details.

If you have a Pay now & Save (pay in app) reservation that you need to modify or cancel, and it’s less than 48 hours before your rental pickup time, please chat with us for support below. It’s important to note that we may direct you to your vehicle supplier if we’re unable to assist you with the changes. If you’re canceling your reservation within 48 hours of the pick-up time, a cancellation fee may apply per your supplier’s terms and conditions. You may also contact your vehicle supplier to make reservation modifications within 48 hours of your current rental pickup time.

Rental FAQs

Do I have to pick up the car or is it dropped off to me?

It depends on which service you select with your reservation. If you opted to pick up the vehicle at the rental counter, you’ll need to pick up and return the vehicle on your own. If you reserve the Car Delivery service available in select markets, the vehicle will be delivered to and collected from a location that you specify in your reservation.

What documents do I need to rent a car?

To rent a car, you typically need the following documents:

  • A valid driver’s license issued in your name
  • A credit card in your name for payment and the security deposit

In some cases, you may need to provide additional documentation such as proof of address. We recommend checking with your rental supplier directly for any additional documents you may need.

What information do I need to provide to rent a car?

To complete your reservation, you must provide the renter’s full name. The name should be between 2-15 characters, and may include Latin and English characters, hyphens, apostrophes, and spaces. Please ensure the name exactly matches the one on your driver’s license and payment method.

What should I bring when picking up the car?

When picking up your rental, bring:

  • Your driver’s license
  • A credit card in your name
  • A photo ID (if required)

In some cases, you may need to provide additional documentation such as proof of address. We recommend checking with your rental supplier directly for any additional documents you may need.

If you are adding additional drivers, they must also be present with their own required documents.

Why do I need to provide a security deposit when renting a car?

A security deposit is a standard practice to cover potential costs for damages, fuel discrepancies, or fines incurred during the rental period. The amount typically ranges from $500 to $1,000 USD, depending on the rental company and vehicle type. The deposit is fully refundable, provided there are no issues at the end of the rental term. Be sure to check the rental terms regarding acceptable forms of payment.

I lost something in the car I rented. What should I do?

To retrieve a missing item, you’ll need to contact the supplier directly and they can assist you with locating your lost items.

What is the minimum age to rent a car?

The minimum age to rent a car generally ranges from 21 to 25, depending on the rental company, location, and service you’re using. If you’re using Car Delivery through Uber, the minimum age requirement is 25. We recommend checking the specific age requirements for the supplier you’ve selected in the app when making the booking.

Can I add an additional driver to the rental agreement?

Yes, additional drivers can be added to your rental agreement at the rental counter, usually with an extra fee. Where there’s an additional driver, all drivers must meet the rental company’s age and licensing criteria. It’s important to inform the rental company of additional drivers when picking up the vehicle. Please remember that additional drivers will need to be physically present with their own documents in order to add them. If you need to add an additional driver for your reservation and you’re using our Car Delivery service, you will need to visit the Avis location in person with the additional driver to add them.

Can I pick up the rental car at one location and return it to another?

Yes! One-way rentals are available at many locations, allowing you to pick up the vehicle at one location and return it to another within the same rental company’s network. Additional fees may apply, and availability varies depending on the locations.

For Car Delivery, you can have different delivery and collection locations as long as both addresses are inside the Car Delivery service area.

How do I return the rental car?

If you picked up the vehicle at the rental counter yourself, you’ll need to bring it back to the designated location at the scheduled time. If you used our Car Delivery service, you’ll need to meet the driver at the location you designated for collection of the car.

In either instance, ensure that the fuel tank is filled to the agreed-upon level, remove all personal belongings, and return the car keys and any provided equipment.

What happens if I need to modify my credit card after making a reservation?

If you’re picking the vehicle up yourself, you can modify your credit card information by contacting the supplier directly. You can find the supplier contact information in the FAQs on your reservation page.

If you’re using our Car Delivery service, Avis will be best suited to assist you. Please call Avis at 800-722-5909, indicate that you wish to replace the payment method on your Avis Preferred account and provide them with your Car Delivery Reservation number.

Is there a mileage limit on rental cars?

Most rental agreements offer unlimited mileage, allowing you to drive without incurring extra charges. However, it’s important to confirm this with your specific rental agreement as some reservations may have mileage limits. You can check for the mileage limit under the Car Details page before completing your booking. There may also be limits on cross-state/cross-country usage, and you may need to confirm with your supplier what they permit.

What should I do if I have an issue with my rental car?

If you encounter any issues with your rental vehicle (such as mechanical problems or cleanliness concerns), please contact the rental company’s customer support team immediately. The helpline number is provided in your rental contract, and we are here to assist you as well.

What is the process for extending my rental period?

To extend your rental, contact the rental company’s customer support or visit a local branch before your original return time. Extensions are subject to availability and will incur additional charges for the extra days. Please note that if you’re using our Car Delivery service, the change will need to be made more than 2.5 hours in advance to ensure that a driver is able to pick up and return the vehicle on your behalf.

Can I request specific features/add-ons or vehicle models?

If you’re picking up the vehicle at the rental counter, you can request specific features & add-ons like child seats or a particular car model at the time of booking. It’s recommended to make these requests in advance to ensure availability. Additional charges may apply for premium models or extra features, such as GPS.

If you are using our Car Delivery service, these features are not available at this time.

What should I do if my document and/or selfie was rejected for a car rental?

If your document or selfie is rejected during the verification process, ensure the following requirements are met:

For documents (driver’s license)
  • Must be a government-issued driver’s license (US licenses are accepted; state IDs, passports, and foreign IDs are not)
  • Ensure the photo is well-lit, clear, and not blurry
  • The license must show your full name, date of birth, ID number, and expiration date
  • No alterations, photos of photos, or photoshopped images
For selfies
  • Take the selfie with no hats or sunglasses (eyeglasses and religious garments are allowed)
  • The photo should show your full face and the top of your shoulders
  • Ensure good lighting, a clear image, and no blurriness
  • Only one person should appear in the photo, and it must be centered, directly facing the camera
  • No filters, photos of photos, or photoshopped images

If you continue to experience issues, please contact our support team for assistance.

Can I rent more than one vehicle at the same time?

No, you are only permitted to rent one vehicle per reservation.

Rental payments

How do I pay for my rental?

Payment options vary depending on the country where you’re renting and the rental supplier.

For rentals in Canada, you may have the option to either:

  • Pay for your rental in the Uber app when you book, or
  • Pay directly to the rental supplier at the rental counter when you collect your vehicle.

The available payment option will be shown during the booking process.

If you choose to pay in the Uber app, you’ll be able to select from the available payment methods in your Uber Wallet, including eligible credit cards, debit cards, and any available Uber Cash.

Regardless of how you pay for your rental, you’ll still need to present a valid physical credit card in the main driver’s name when you collect the vehicle. The rental supplier will typically place a security deposit on the card, which is separate from the rental price and is generally refunded after the vehicle is returned, provided there are no additional charges, such as damage, fuel or charging fees, or unpaid tolls or fines.

Rentals in other countries

Payment methods vary depending on the country, rental supplier, and reservation. Some reservations can be paid directly to the rental supplier at the rental counter, while others can be prepaid in the Uber app. The available payment option for your reservation will always be shown during the booking process.

Rental pricing

How is the rental price calculated?

We compare prices and available vehicles from multiple rental suppliers to help you find the best options. Rental prices are set by each supplier and may vary depending on factors such as seasonality, vehicle availability, rental duration, and your selected pickup and drop-off locations.

The price displayed at the time of booking is the upfront rental price provided by the supplier. Depending on your reservation, you may pay for your rental in the Uber app or directly to the rental supplier at pickup. This price assumes the vehicle is returned in accordance with the rental agreement and without any additional charges. The final amount you pay may change if you modify your reservation or incur additional charges during your rental, such as optional extras, fuel or charging fees, damage, tolls, fines, or other charges determined by the rental supplier.

Security deposit

The rental supplier will typically require a security deposit when you collect your vehicle. This is standard practice across the car rental industry and is separate from the rental price. The deposit helps cover potential charges that may arise during the rental, such as vehicle damage, missing fuel or charging, unpaid tolls or fines, or other charges outlined in the supplier’s rental agreement.

The security deposit amount is determined by the rental supplier and varies depending on the supplier and vehicle type. For smaller vehicles, it is often under $500 although the required amount may be higher depending on the vehicle or supplier.

Most rental suppliers require the security deposit to be paid using a physical credit card, and some only accept certain card brands. We recommend reviewing the supplier’s Terms and Conditions before booking to confirm the required payment method and applicable deposit amount.

The supplier will generally refund the security deposit after the vehicle is returned, provided there are no outstanding charges or damage identified at the end of the rental.

Car Delivery

If you’re using Car Delivery (available in select U.S. cities only), an additional delivery fee applies for the vehicle to be delivered to and collected from your selected location. This fee is included in the total price shown during booking.

Pricing may vary depending on factors such as the delivery distance, promotions, or other applicable conditions.

Looking for a vehicle to drive and earn with Uber?

Uber Rent offers cars for personal use only and can’t be used to earn on the Uber platform.

If you’d like a vehicle for driving and earning, please visit the Uber Vehicle Marketplace in the app or online.

Supplier FAQs

What is the deposit amount I will need to make on my car rental?

Security deposits are fully managed and set by the rental car companies. The deposit amount will vary based on certain factors such as the age of the renter, type of vehicle, length of rental, and more.

Deposits will require a credit card authorization at the time of rental start, and will either be removed or result in a charge at the end of the rental period, all determined by the rental car companies.

A security deposit is a standard practice to cover potential costs for damages, fuel discrepancies, or fines incurred during the rental period. The amount typically ranges from $500 to $1,000 USD, depending on the rental company and vehicle type. The deposit is fully refundable, provided there are no issues at the end of the rental term. Be sure to check the rental terms regarding acceptable forms of payment.

Which suppliers can I rent from?

We’re proud to offer vehicles from various providers, including Avis, Budget, Hertz, Sixt, Thrifty, and Dollar Rent-A-Car.

What is the contact information for my supplier?

Uber partners with over 1700 rental companies worldwide and therefore we cannot list all providers. However, here is the contact information for our most frequently booked providers:

Resources for riders with disabilities

Accessibility at Uber

State and federal laws prohibit discrimination against individuals with disabilities, including those accompanied by service animals. Drivers using the platform agree to follow all applicable laws and Uber’s policies, which state that drivers may not deny service to or otherwise discriminate against riders with disabilities.

Any report of unlawful discrimination or ride refusal may result in temporarily deactivating a driver’s account while we review the incident.

Additionally, drivers are expected to assist riders using walkers, canes, folding wheelchairs, or other assistive devices to the maximum extent possible.

Can I schedule a Uber WAV trip?

At this time, you cannot request the vehicle option WAV using the schedule a ride feature.

If Uber WAV is available in your city, you can request a trip when you are ready to ride. You can click on the link below to see if Uber WAV is available and the steps for how to request a trip.

Cleaning Fees for Service Animals

Riders will:

  • Be refunded any cleaning fees charged for shedding by their service animals.
  • Not be charged for the first or second reported mess involving a service animal’s bodily fluids. You can be charged for the third reported mess involving a service animal’s bodily fluids.

Riders may contest that such a mess occurred by responding to the fee notification email. If a rider contests the cleaning fee, Uber will make a reasonable good faith effort to determine whether a mess occurred.

This policy does not affect a driver’s rights to their own service animals.

How to Ride with WAV and Request Reimbursement

Uber is now offering reimbursement for wheelchair-accessible (WAV) rides. If you recently took a (WAV) trip within the Vancouver Lower Mainland and Fraser Valley areas in a taxicab unaffiliated with Uber, you may be eligible for a reimbursement.

Reimbursement is only available for trips completed within the Vancouver Lower Mainland and Fraser Valley areas, including:

  • Abbotsford
  • Chilliwack
  • City of Burnaby
  • City of Coquitlam
  • City of Delta
  • City of Langley
  • City of Maple Ridge
  • City of New Westminster
  • City of North Vancouver
  • City of Pitt Meadows
  • City of Port Coquitlam
  • City of Port Moody
  • City of Richmond
  • City of Surrey
  • City of Vancouver
  • City of White Rock
  • District of North Vancouver
  • District of West Vancouver
  • Harrison Hot Springs
  • Kent
  • Township of Langley
  • Tsawwassen First Nation.

How to request WAV

Open the app and enter your destination in the “Where to?” box. Once you confirm your pickup and destination addresses are correct, select WAV at the bottom of your screen. Then tap Confirm WAV.

A message will pop up with a link to a website where you can find contact details to request a wheelchair-accessible ride from local accessible taxicab options.

For fare difference reimbursement: Take a screenshot that clearly shows the pick-up and drop-off locations, along with the UberX fare.

How to be Reimbursed

  1. Fill out all the information in this form.
  2. Send your taxi trip receipt and a screenshot of the UberX fare to wav-canada-group@uber.com.
  3. Make sure to include the date and time of your trip in the subject line and the pick-up and drop-off locations in the email body so we can verify your trip details.

Your taxi receipt should clearly show the fare, tips, taxes, and the pick-up and drop-off locations. The screenshot should show the UberX fare along with the pick-up and drop-off locations from the Uber app.

You will receive your reimbursement within 45 days through your preferred reimbursement method such as Uber Voucher. Tips are excluded from reimbursement. Trips completed more than 90 days ago are not eligible for reimbursement.

Please note: Uber reserves the right to withhold or deduct reimbursement payments that it determines or believes were fraudulent, illegal, in error, or in violation of terms or the agreement(s) between you and Uber.

No, tips are excluded from reimbursement

Trips that were completed more than 90 days ago are not eligible for reimbursement.

You’ll receive your reimbursement within 45 days after submitting the required documents and sufficient information.

Can we help with anything else?

Service Animal Self-ID

Uber is committed to safety and responsible experiences for our communities. Transparent, upfront disclosures about service animals help improve the experience between riders and drivers.

Uber’s Self-ID feature allows riders to notify drivers in-app when a service animal may be present during a trip. Riders can choose when to disclose to the driver that they are traveling with a service animal:

  • No disclosure: Drivers won’t see any service animal indication in the rider’s profile.
  • Disclosure at pickup: Drivers will be notified upon arrival at the pickup location.

For more information, visit our Service animal user guide and Service Animal and Assistive Device Policy.

To access the eligibility form, go to Account > Settings > Accessibility in your app profile settings.

To report a service animal denial, please fill out the report a service animal issue form.

Can we help with anything else?

What is Assist?

Assist is a vehicle option that offers door-to-door assistance for those who require additional assistance.

Assist vehicles can accommodate:

  • Folding wheelchairs
  • Walkers
  • Collapsible scooters

Assist vehicles do not have wheelchair-accessible ramps or lifts.

Drivers are trained by accredited training centers on how to assist you with disability and mobility challenges, such as how to help transfer you safely from a wheelchair to the car.

Drivers are not required to provide door-through-frame assistance, such as helping someone inside their home or walking them in to a building.

What is WAV?

WAV is a vehicle option available in some markets that offers riders who use non-folding, motorized wheelchairs to connect with drivers in wheelchair accessible vehicles that are equipped with ramps or lifts.

  • WAV drivers are certified by a third party in wheelchair securement and assisting people with disabilities.
  • If there are drivers with WAV in your market, you can request one directly through the app just like UberX.

In some markets in the United States and Canada, we provide information on unaffiliated WAV providers.

Ride Passes

Prepaid Pass

What is Prepaid Pass?

Prepaid Pass allows you to purchase a set number of rides (e.g., 5, 10, 15, or 20 trips) for a specific route and ride type (UberX only for now, more vehicles to be added in the near future) at a locked-in price per trip. This means you pay upfront and don’t have to worry about price surges for those rides within your selected bundle.

How does Prepaid Pass work?

  1. Choose your route: Select a single pickup and drop-off point. We might also suggest routes based on your past trips.
  2. Pick your ride type: Currently available for UberX only
  3. Select a daily request window: Choose a 1-hour time slot each day when your pass will be active.
  4. Choose your bundle size: Decide how many trips you want to buy (5, 10, 15, or 20).
  5. Pay upfront: Pay for your bundle using digital payment methods. Uber Cash or Uber One credits can also be used.
  6. Ride with predictability: When you request a ride within your chosen route, ride type, and request window, your Prepaid Pass will automatically apply, and you’ll pay $0 for that trip .

NOTE: You’ll need the latest version of the app in order to use Prepaid Pass.

What are the benefits of Prepaid Pass?

  • Price Predictability: Say goodbye to surge pricing for your bundled trips! You’ll know exactly what you’re paying per ride upfront.
  • Potential Savings: Depending on the bundle size and your location, you could save money compared to paying for individual rides, especially during peak times.
  • Budgeting Made Easy: Plan your transportation expenses for the week or month with a fixed upfront cost.
  • Convenience: Once purchased, your pass automatically applies when you meet the criteria, making your daily commute smoother.

Who is Prepaid Pass for?

Prepaid Pass is designed for anyone who takes repeat trips, especially commuters who are looking for more price stability and predictability. It’s also being developed with features to benefit Uber for Teens and Uber for Family users.

How long is a Prepaid Pass valid?

The validity period for your pass will be specified at the time of purchase. We are currently testing different validity windows (e.g., 4 or 8 weeks).

Can I have multiple Prepaid Passes?

Yes, you can have multiple Prepaid Passes for different routes as long as they are for the same product type (e.g., all UberX passes). If you have multiple active passes for an overlapping route and product, the pass expiring soonest will be used first.

You can have up to 10 distinct (unique pickup/dropoff) active passes at a time.

What if I’m an Uber One member?

Existing Uber One members can still earn Uber One credits on their Prepaid Pass rides! The exact percentage will be displayed at checkout. We are also exploring future benefits for Uber One members, such as longer expiration windows or better pricing.

What payment methods can I use?

Currently, you can use any digital payment method supported by Uber, including Uber Cash or Uber One credits, to purchase your pass. We plan to introduce cash payments in the future.

What happens if I cancel a trip?

If you cancel a trip, your Prepaid Pass trip token will not be used. However, standard cancellation fees will still apply according to existing policies in your market.

Can I change my trip details after purchasing a pass?

If you make changes to your trip (like adding a stop or changing the pickup/drop-off location outside the pass’s defined route), your Prepaid Pass will generally not apply, and you will be charged the real-time rate for that ride. We will warn you in the future if a change will make your pass ineligible.

Will this affect my driver’s earnings?

No, driver earnings are not affected by Prepaid Pass. Drivers will earn their standard fare for each trip, with any difference between your locked-in price and the real-time fare being handled by Uber.

How will I see my Prepaid Pass in the app?

You’ll see a clear badge on the product selector when a Prepaid Pass is active for a specific ride. Your trip receipt will show “$0 - You redeemed a prepaid ride” for trips where the pass was applied. You’ll also be able to manage and view all your active and expired passes within the app.

What if I have more questions?

For specific questions about your Prepaid Membership, reach out to Uber Support.

Can we help with anything else?

Price lock pass

Price Lock Pass (a “Pass”) is an auto-renewing subscription that gives you the convenience of securing a maximum price for a route and one-hour time frame that you choose for the month. You can sign-up for multiple Passes if you want to secure pricing for different routes or even the same route at different times of day.

By subscribing for just $2.99 per month per Pass, you can lock in a maximum price for your chosen route and one-hour time frame each day (“Locked Price.”). For 30 days, your Locked Price applies to your selected route, provided you order the ride within the specific hour you chose. When you go to request a ride, if the real-time price is lower than the Locked Price, you will pay the lower price. Each month, each Pass will renew at $2.99, however, based on market conditions, your Locked Price may adjust. Uber will provide you with your updated Locked Price for each Pass seven (7) days before it renews, so you can cancel your subscription and avoid the $2.99 monthly charge if you prefer. For example, in one month your Locked Price might be $15.49, and in the next month it could be $16.49 (excluding the $2.99 subscription fee). You’re free to cancel a Pass at any time to avoid future monthly charges. If you want to change the route or time for a future month, you’ll need to cancel that Pass and sign up for a new one. If you cancel your Pass before the end of the 30 days, you will continue to receive your Locked Price until the 30 days have concluded. After that, your Pass will not renew and you will pay the market rate for rides.

Each month you can save a maximum of $50 per Pass, not including the $2.99 price of the subscription. Savings are calculated by comparing the real-time price when you request a ride to the maximum price you locked for the month. Using your benefits impact just the price, and will impact the time it takes for a ride request to be accepted by a driver. If the real-time price for the ride is lower than your locked price, your benefit isn’t used and no cost is added towards the $50 limit. After reaching $50 of savings in a month, any subsequent rides will be charged at the real-time price. Until you cancel, your Pass will automatically renew each month and your $50 savings limit resets.

This service is currently available exclusively in a few Uber markets across the United States. We are excited to slowly roll it out to all Uber markets in the United States.

Managing Your Passes

To oversee your current Passes or to add new ones, simply navigate to the Price Lock section in the app menu. Here you can view details of existing Passes, check their statuses, and manage auto-renewal preferences. To purchase an additional Pass, select Add a Price Lock Pass and follow the steps to set it up.

Need to make adjustments?

Once a Pass is purchased, it cannot be modified. If you want to change the route or time, you’ll need to cancel the Pass and then purchase a new one with the updated details. To cancel, please navigate to “Ride Passes” under the Account section of the app, choose the Pass you would like to cancel and simply click “Cancel Pass” and follow the prompts.

How to Enroll

Follow these steps to set up your Price Lock Pass using the app: 1. Navigate to the Ride Passes or Commute Hub option in the Account Hub section of the App 2. Enter and confirm your desired pickup and dropoff points 3. Choose a one-hour daily window 4. Review the product benefits and terms. Note, here you will have the chance to purchase Price Lock Pass or other types of non-renewing products to save on this route and time. 5. Complete your payment for your auto-renewing subscription

Once the $2.99 subscription fee is processed, your Pass is active and your Pass price will appear for ride requests for the locked route and time.

Need Further Help?

If you have any questions or need assistance, our team is happy to help. Please reach out to us.

Can we help with anything else?

Safety

Audio Recording

Turn on Audio Recording for an added layer of safety on every trip

We hope that every trip goes well, but we also know that sometimes things don’t go as planned. With the Audio Recording safety feature enabled, your trips will be automatically recorded in the Uber app.

How to opt-in

Allow microphone access for the Uber app. You will be prompted to do this when setting up your Teen account for the first time. You may later return to your settings if you wish to turn the feature off.

How recording works

Recording will start as soon as your driver is nearing your pickup location. Recording will continue throughout the trip and will end 20 seconds after the end of your ride.

Your privacy is always protected

All audio recordings are encrypted and stored securely on your device. Your recording will be locked, and no one—not your driver, not Uber, not you or your guardian—has access to it. Uber can only access the recording if you report a safety incident to Uber Support and choose to share the recording.

Your driver may also choose to record the audio of your trip, and the same privacy standards apply. This means your driver also won’t be able to listen to any of their recordings. If they have Audio Recording set up, you’ll be notified while they’re on their way to pick you up so that you know before your trip starts.

How to report an issue

To send audio to Uber Support in the event of a safety incident, go to Account > Trips and choose the trip you want to report. You’ll be prompted to describe what happened in as much detail as you can and a member of the Uber Support Team will contact you once they have reviewed the submitted recording.

Emergency contact information

What is an emergency contact?

An emergency contact is a person you choose that can be contacted if a specific incident occurs and Uber is unable to contact you directly.

When will Uber contact this person?

In case of emergency, Uber will first try to contact you, and if that’s not possible, we will call the emergency contacts you have chosen.

If we call your emergency contact, the subject could be:

Investigative: To help to compile information about the emergency incident, including what happened and who were involved.

Informative: To inform the contact about what happened in the emergency incident.

These are the events that Uber has defined as emergencies:

  • Death
  • Hospitalization
  • Ambulance needed to be called
  • Serious injuries

Although they are extremely rare, these situations can occur during a trip.

You can add any number as an emergency contact, but Uber will only try to contact up to 2 contacts, giving priority to the last 2 registered.

If Uber manages to communicate with the first emergency contact, we will not contact the second one.

And, what about privacy?

Uber is committed to the User data privacy. Your contacts will only be called in case of emergency. If you delete an emergency contact from your account, Uber will delete this information right away.

I was involved in an accident

We are committed to the well-being of everyone using the app. If you have been involved in an incident, please make sure that you and all parties involved are out of harm’s way. Immediately notify police and paramedics if necessary.

Please let us know what happened by sharing details that will help us to support you. We will get in touch.

My driver didn’t match the profile in my app

For every trip, your Uber app helps you recognize the vehicle and driver you ride with. After your ride request is accepted, your app displays your driver’s name, vehicle make and model, and license plate number.

If the driver for this trip was not who you were expecting, please let us know here.

Signing up

Downloading the rider app

Android download steps

You can download and install the Uber rider app on Android devices with operating system 8.0 or higher, and iOS devices with iOS 15.0 or higher. Head to the Google Play store and follow these steps:

  1. Tap the Google Play icon.
  2. Tap the search bar, type Uber, then tap Search.
  3. Select the Uber icon and tap INSTALL.
  4. After app installation is complete, tap OPEN.
  5. In the Uber app, tap SIGN IN if you have an account or tap REGISTER to create one.

WINDOWS

Beginning in June 2018, the Uber app will no longer be available on Windows devices. Windows users can request a ride via m.uber.com.

Downloading the rider app (iOS)

You can download and install the Uber rider app on devices with iOS 8 and newer. Open the App Store on your mobile device, then follow the steps below.

  1. Tap “Search” in the menu bar at the bottom, then type “Uber” in the search bar
  2. Select “Uber” from the search results
  3. Next to the Uber icon, tap “Get.” You may be required to enter your Apple ID or password.
  4. When the download is complete, “Get” will change to “Open.” Tap “Open” to open the app.
  5. In the Uber app, tap “Sign in” if you have an account, or “Register” to create an account

Error adding a payment method

If the CVV number or billing zip code for your credit or debit card is entered incorrectly, you may receive a message about an error processing your payment after a trip.

Try removing the payment method, then add it again, making sure all info is correct.

If you continue to receive messages about payment errors, contact your financial institution to verify that your payment account is active and verified internationally.

How do I create an Uber account?

Creating an Uber account requires a valid email address and phone number. You’ll also need to create a password and agree to terms and conditions and our privacy notice.

  1. Fill in your first and last name, phone number, and preferred language.
  2. Once you complete this part of the sign-up process, we send a text SMS to verify your phone number.
  3. Next, enter your payment information. Adding a credit card or debit card number allows your trip fares to be automatically charged after each ride.
  4. After you provide this info, we’ll send an email to confirm your account registration.

How does Uber work?

Uber is a technology platform. Our smartphone apps connect drivers and riders.

In cities where Uber operates, use the app to request a ride. When a nearby driver accepts your request, the app displays an estimated time of arrival for the driver heading to your pickup location. The app notifies you when the driver is about to arrive.

The app also provides info about the driver with whom you will ride, including first name, vehicle type, and license plate number. This info helps the two of you connect at your pickup location.

Use the app to enter your preferred destination anytime before or during the ride. If you have a preferred route, it’s helpful to talk through the directions together.

When you arrive at your destination and exit the vehicle, your trip ends. Your fare is automatically calculated and charged to the payment method you’ve linked to your Uber account.

In some cities, Uber allows you to pay your fare in cash. This option must be selected before you request a ride.

Immediately after a trip ends, the app will ask you to rate your driver from 1 to 5 Stars. Drivers are also asked to rate riders. Uber’s feedback system is designed to foster a community of respect and accountability for everyone.

Learn more about how Uber works by exploring other Help Center topics. You can search for specific questions and answers too.

Why do I need to verify my account using Facebook?

Why account verification is required

To help keep rides stress-free and secure, Uber may ask you to confirm your identity when you sign up or update your payment method.

Ways to verify your account
  • Connect your verified Facebook account
  • Add a verified digital payment method (such as PayPal or another payment method)
If you sign up using a gift card

You’ll need to verify your account by either: - Adding a digital payment method - Signing into your verified Facebook account through the Uber app

  1. Select Use Facebook in the Uber app
  2. Log into your Facebook account
  3. Select “Public Profile (required)” or “Friend List”
  4. Select “Continue” to finish verification
