If you don’t have a Facebook account

You can either create a Facebook account or add a digital payment method to verify your Uber account.

Turo on Uber

About Turo on Uber

Turo on Uber is an exciting new partnership that provides Renters access to Turo’s vehicle base accessible for booking through the Uber platform. This unique offering allows users who are already using the Uber platform to skip the rental counters and book Rental vehicles directly with Turo hosts.

Booking a vehicle with a Turo host through Uber means that users will be able to unlock benefits that are not available with other rental options, including:

  • The ability to tailor the rental to exactly what you want, meaning you’ll know everything about the vehicle you’re driving ahead of time including the year, make, model, and even the color.
  • Choose from a wide selection of vehicles - from everyday cars to luxury options - so you can find the perfect ride for any occasion.
  • The ability to select a meeting spot with the Turo host. You won’t have to wait in a rental counter line: instead, you’ll pick from a list of available exchange locations and specify a time that’s best for your rental exchange.
  • The ability to use your Uber benefits alongside the flexibility of booking a Turo trip through Uber. You’ll be able to use your Uber Cash to book vehicles, and you may even be eligible for promotions based on your Uber One Membership Status.

You can check out Turo’s Help Center page for more detailed information about Turo on Uber. But if you don’t wish to use Turo on Uber and you’d rather rent a traditional Self-Pickup (at the rental counter) or Car Delivery, please consider checking out the Rentals by Uber section of our Help Center.

If you have questions about Turo on Uber and haven’t made a booking yet? Chat with us below:

Active Turo bookings and trips

If you’re currently on a Turo trip or you’re reaching out about a confirmed/active booking, our support agents are unable to assist you directly.

For assistance with your active reservation, please contact Turo Support to get the help you need.

Canceling or changing an existing Turo reservation

If your Turo reservation is still pending and it’s been less that 60 minutes since you placed the booking, you’ll be able to cancel in the Uber app by following these steps:

  1. Find your reservations in the Uber app and open it
  2. Scroll down and select Cancel Reservation
  3. Select a cancellation reason when prompted
  4. Confirm your cancellation

You should get a confirmation email indicating that your Turo on Uber reservation was canceled.

However, if you don’t see the Cancel Reservation button your reservation may already be confirmed by Turo. In those cases, you’ll need to reach out to Turo Support for further assistance with canceling your reservation. Since they handle post-booking issues they can guide you on topics like how to modify or cancel your reservation, and they can also help connect you to the Turo host if needed.

You can head to our About Turo on Uber page for more detailed information about Turo on Uber. And if you don’t want to use Turo on Uber and you’d rather rent a traditional Self-Pickup (at the rental counter) or Car Delivery, please consider checking out the Rentals by Uber section of our Help Center.

This will include modifications to the booking, cancellation of the booking, or any on-trip concerns (such as concerns with vehicle pickup/exchange).

Pickups and dropoffs

Typically, you can select your preferred pickup and return locations when searching for a vehicle. You and the host will also usually be able to further coordinate these details of the reservation together. There are options to have the Turo host meet you with the vehicle at a pre-selected location or to have a Turo host deliver the vehicle to you, which may incur additional fees.

There may also be fees for pickups or dropoffs at the airport or at paid parking facilities. This is subject to change depending on updates to Turo’s terms of service and product offerings.

For more information about how a pickup/dropoff might work for your specific reservation, please contact Turo Support directly.

Can we help with anything else?

Safety issues

If you experience a safety issue during your trip, your immediate safety is the top priority. If you’re in danger or need urgent assistance, please call emergency services right away.

For any safety-related concerns with the vehicle or the host (including mechanical issues, host concerns, or if you’ve been in an accident), you’ll need to contact Turo immediately so they can assist with resolving the issue and ensure your safety. They can guide you through the next steps and help determine the best course of action. An Uber agent won’t be able to assist you with a Turo related safety concern.

You can click here to get in contact with Turo now.

Can we help with anything else?

Turo FAQs

There are a substantial number of FAQs that Turo can answer directly through their Help Center. However, listed below are some commonly asked questions and answers. Please keep in mind that in most cases reaching out to Turo Support directly will be necessary.

Modifying an existing trip/reservation

To modify an existing trip or reservation, you can typically make changes through the Turo app, such as adjusting the dates or modifying your reservation details. However, not all changes can be made directly by users and there may be restrictions depending on the timing of your request and the specific circumstances.

For assistance with making modifications to your trip, you’ll need to contact Turo support. You can reach out to them directly by going to their Help Center for more detailed guidance and to ensure your request is processed appropriately.

Roadside assistance

If you require roadside assistance during your trip, Turo provides support through their partnered services. Depending on the situation, you may be able to access help for issues like flat tires, lockouts, or breakdowns.

To get assistance, you’ll need to contact Turo directly through their Help Center for further instructions and to ensure that the appropriate services are dispatched to your location and the host is notified appropriately.

Vehicle has a mechanical issue and/or needs maintenance

If you encounter a mechanical issue with the vehicle or need maintenance during your trip, it’s important to address the situation promptly. Depending on the nature of the issue, Turo may offer support or guidance on how to handle the situation.

For assistance, you’ll need to contact Turo directly through their Help Center so they can help assess the problem and advise you on the next steps to take.

I can’t get in contact with the host

If you’re unable to get in touch with your host, it’s important to try reaching out through the Turo platform to ensure your message is delivered. If you’ve already done this and still haven’t received a response, you’ll need to contact Turo support for assistance by going to their Help Center.

Turo will help mediate the situation and ensure that you can proceed with your reservation or resolve any issues you’re facing. At this time, Uber cannot assist in connecting renters with hosts.

Pickups and dropoffs

If you choose the delivery option during the booking process, the host will deliver the vehicle to your location. If you did not select vehicle delivery, you will be responsible for picking up the vehicle from the host at the pickup location listed in the confirmation details or your reservations page on the Turo app.

To find if your reservation is for delivery or pickup, please navigate to the reservation in the Turo app. If you have questions about the pickup or delivery availability based on your location, please contact Turo directly.

Necessary documents for Turo rentals

To rent a car, you typically need the following documents:

  • A valid driver’s license issued in your name
  • A credit card in your name for payment and the security deposit
  • In some cases you may need to provide additional documentation, such as proof of address

We recommend checking with Turo directly for any additional documents you may need once your booking is completed. For more information, please visit the Turo Help Center.

I want to sign up to be a host with Turo

To sign up as a Host on Turo, you’ll need to create an account on their platform and follow the steps to list your vehicle(s). You’ll be guided through the process of providing details about your car, uploading photos, and setting your pricing and availability.

For more information on becoming a Host and the requirements involved, you’ll need to visit the Turo website or contact Turo support directly for assistance. Uber will be unable to assist you with this specific inquiry.

Uber Cash

Purchasing Uber Cash

Uber Cash can be purchased directly through your Uber app. To do this:

  1. Select Account in the bottom right corner of your app.
  2. Choose Wallet and then Add Funds.
  3. Pick the amount you’d like to purchase.
  4. Select Payment Method to choose how you’d like to buy.
  5. Tap Purchase.

How to enable/disable auto-refill:

  1. Tap on Account in the bottom right corner of your app.
  2. Tap Wallet to see your current Uber Cash balance
  3. Select Add funds and then tap on Auto-Refill.
  4. Choose the amount added to your balance each time it drops below $10.
  5. Toggle Auto-Refill on/off.
  6. Tap Update.

What happens if I delete my account?

When your account is deleted, you will be emailed a PIN you can redeem in the future for the Uber Cash balance you previously purchased.

Please note that promotional credits, along with other amounts of Uber Cash that were not purchased will be permanently forfeited if your account is deleted.

If you’re having an issue purchasing Uber Cash, please share the details with us below.

For more information, please visit this page.

Redeeming Uber Cash

Uber Cash appears in your app as the combined balance of any gift cards, credits given by Uber Support, promotional credits, Amex Premium Benefits, or Uber Cash purchases.

While it can be applied to most rides and Uber Eats orders, it’s not available for trips taken on Family Profiles or requested from the web.

If you meant to use Uber Cash on a previous trip or Uber Eats order, it’s possible to switch your payment, though you’ll need enough Uber Cash on your account to cover the full price of the transaction.

When requesting a ride your balance will automatically be applied, unless you are using a business profile.

To manually enable Uber Cash on a business profile, or turn it off and save it for later on your rides profile, you’ll need to review your payment options. To do this:

  1. Enter your destination.
  2. Before confirming your ride request, tap your current payment method.
  3. If you have a profile set up, select your payment method again to adjust the Uber Cash toggle on or off.
  4. Return to the previous screen and request your ride.

When you don’t have enough Uber Cash to cover a trip, the remaining cost will be charged to the primary payment method on your account. To use Uber Cash, you need to have an active payment method selected and added to your account.

If you still have questions about using Uber Cash or need assistance, please connect with us below.

Refund of Uber Cash Purchase

Any Uber Cash that wasn’t purchased is non-refundable. This includes: * Promotional credits earned * Amounts received from customer support

If you have unused Uber Cash from a purchase, you may be able to request a refund.

If you’re facing issues and need assistance, please check out our Uber Cash FAQ or connect with us below and we’ll be happy to take a look.

For more information, please visit 0

Uber for business travel

Creating a business ride profile

Business ride profiles allow you to charge rides to your business credit card and receive receipts at your work email address. You can join your company’s business account or create a business ride profile on your own.

To create a business ride profile:

  1. Open the Uber app and tap “Account.”
  2. Tap “Wallet,” then scroll down to “Ride Profiles.”
  3. Tap “Start using Uber for business” or “Add another business profile.”
  4. Follow the prompts to create your business profile.
  5. Optional: Select your company’s expense provider to enable automatic receipt forwarding (you’ll receive an email to complete the linking process). To add an expense provider later, see How to edit a personal or business ride profile.

You must select your business ride profile before or during any ride that you want charged to your business credit card.

To join your company’s business account, tap the link below for instructions.

Delete my business profile

To delete your business profile in the Uber app:

  1. Tap “Account.”
  2. Select “Payment” or “Wallet” and scroll down to “Ride Profiles.”
  3. Tap “Business” to open the business profile.
  4. Tap the bin icon and then “Delete.”

For Uber for Business accounts managed by your company, you must contact the account administrator who can delete your account at business.uber.com.

Once a business profile is deleted, you can no longer take trips on that profile or receive travel reports.

If you’re having trouble deleting your business profile, fill out the form below and we’ll be in touch.

How to use expense memos

Uber for Business account admins can require expense codes for trips taken on the business profile.

If your admin required expense codes, you’ll be prompted to select an expense code from a list or enter your own before requesting a ride.

If you aren’t prompted to but want to enter an expense code or memo, you can do so in the Expense info tab at the bottom of your screen after you’ve requested a ride.

Note: The expense memo must be filled out before the trip ends. You can’t edit expense info after a trip is complete.

Expense codes will be noted in your monthly statement and trip receipts. Your company’s admins also have access to this info in their dashboard. Ask your admin about your company’s expense memo policy.

Requesting a trip on a business profile

Once you’ve joined your company’s business account, you’re ready to start requesting rides on the business profile.

  1. Open the app.
  2. Tap “Where to?” to edit pickup address (if needed) and enter your destination address.
  3. Underneath the “Vehicle type” selector, tap the toggle to switch between personal and business profiles.
  4. To select a different business profile (if you have more than one) or different payment method, tap the arrow next to the profile name.
  5. Confirm your pickup location and, if prompted, select an expense code.
  6. Request your ride.

Your company won’t see any information about rides taken on your personal profile.

Your organization’s admin can create rules and restrictions around when and where employees use the account. If you can’t request a trip and think it’s a mistake, please contact your admin.

Need more information on requesting a ride or switching between ride profiles?

Resolve a flagged trip

If you got an app notification and/or email asking for more information on a business trip you took, someone at your company may have flagged your trip for being out of policy.

You’ll need to either switch the trip to your personal profile, or explain to your company why the trip fell within policy in the message box provided. You can do both directly in the app.

Switching between personal and business ride profiles

You can switch between your personal and business profiles either before or during a trip.

Before a trip:

  1. Tap “Where to?” to edit your pickup location (if needed) and enter your destination.
  2. Tap the toggle beneath the vehicle options to switch between personal and business profiles.
  3. To select a different profile, tap the profile name and select the profile you want to use.

During a trip:

  1. Tap the arrow next to your driver’s information.
  2. Select “Change payment.”
  3. Choose whether you want to switch to a business or personal profile. If you have multiple business profiles, select the one you want the trip charged to.

Your app will default to the profile used for your last ride.

If a trip ended and was charged to the wrong payment method, you can switch your payment method.

Tipping on business rides

Whether you’ll pay for tips on business rides depends on your company’s expense policy.

If your company covers the full cost of trips, any tips to drivers will be charged to the company account.

If you were asked to add a second payment method when you joined your company’s business account, the administrator on the account set rules around spending allowances. This means that ride payments will be charged to the company’s account, but tips and ride payments that exceed the spending allowance will be charged to your personal payment method.

Tipping is not available in all countries.

Need information on tipping with vouchers?

Using Uber for Business as an employee

Uber for Business works with your existing Uber rider account, allowing you to separate your travel for personal, business, or other purposes by selecting the appropriate profile you wish to travel with.

If you’re joining your company’s business account, you’ll accept an email invitation by creating an Uber account or signing in to your existing Uber account. Visit the article below for more details.

Your company’s account admin has access to a dashboard that displays the following information about trips charged to the account:

  • Pickup location and destination
  • Selected vehicle option
  • Date and time of the trip’s start and end
  • Trip duration
  • Applied expense code (if applicable)

NOTE: The company admin will never see details about trips taken on your personal profile.

Each time you take a trip using your company’s Uber for Business account, you might have to enter an expense code and memo (if the admin set rules for doing so).

For more information on expense codes and memos, see the article below.

If you have questions on using the account, please reach out to the company admin.

If you’re new to Uber, visit the help section below for tips on using the app.

What is Business Comfort?

Business Comfort is a ride option that was designed with corporate travelers in mind. Whether you need to relax or get work done, Business Comfort provides the extra legroom and a stress-free environment optimized for business travelers in select markets.

Benefits

  • Business Comfort is an exclusive ride option available only to those taking trips on an Uber for Business profile
  • More spacious rides give you the extra comfort you need to work or relax on the road
  • Choose custom preferences in advance to tailor your ride such as a quiet car or a cooler temperature
  • Highly rated drivers so you can expect a smoother ride
  • Premium support with 24/7 availability
  • Priority pickup in select markets

Uber for delivery

Package delivery FAQ

What is Courier?

Courier is an option that allows you to request a driver to help you transport your package(s) to the person waiting at the designated drop-off location.

What can I send?

For packages delivered by vehicle, you can send small or medium packages that:

  • Don’t contain any prohibited items (see list of prohibited items below)
  • Have a combined maximum weight of 30 pounds
  • Fit comfortably in the trunk of a mid-size vehicle
  • Are closed, securely sealed, and ready for curbside or door pickup
  • May not have a total value greater than $200 USD

For packages delivered by bike or scooter, you can send small or medium packages that:

  • Don’t contain any prohibited items (see list of prohibited items below)
  • Have a combined maximum weight of 15 pounds
  • Fit comfortably in a backpack
  • Are closed, securely sealed, and ready for curbside or door pickup
  • May not have a total value greater than $200 USD

If your package contains a prohibited item or doesn’t follow the restrictions above, the driver may cancel your request.

How to request a delivery

  1. Enter the package destination in the “Where to?” section of your Uber app and ensure the pickup location is correct. You won’t be able to change the pickup or dropoff address after requesting the delivery.
  2. Scroll through the vehicle options and select “Courier.”
  3. Review your payment method and tap “Next.”
  4. Agree to the package delivery Terms and Conditions, and confirm that your package doesn’t contain any prohibited items.
  5. Reply to the message you’ll receive in the Uber app asking for the recipient’s name and any special delivery instructions for your driver.
  6. Meet the driver at the door or curbside depending on the pickup/delivery method selected and load the package into the vehicle.
  7. Instruct the recipient to meet the driver at the door or curbside depending on the pickup/delivery method selected and retrieve the package from the driver’s vehicle.

Note: You can send more than one package at one time. This feature is at the bottom of the requested trip card and there is also an activity hub that will display all active trips for one user. You may see the option to batch your package delivery with other packages along the same route for a lower price.

Note: The package recipient should be available to meet the driver at the door or curbside depending on the pickup/delivery method selected. If you need to ask the driver to leave the package at the recipient’s door, you must include those instructions as part of the special delivery instructions or in a message to the driver within the app.

If your package was damaged during delivery and you would like to seek a refund of the delivery cost, you must submit a photo and description of the damage within three business days following the delivery date.

Uber does not maintain insurance for packages. Please see the Terms and Conditions for full details. Violation of the Terms and Conditions may result in deactivation of your account without notice.

Can I send someone a package as a surprise?

We recommend that you notify the recipient of the delivery so they can meet the driver at the door or curb depending on the pickup/delivery method selected, to retrieve the package from the vehicle.

If you do send someone a package as a surprise you’ll need to clearly instruct the driver, in the message section of the Uber app, to leave the package at the recipient’s door. The driver can always decline this request.

SMS Support for Package Delivery

If you received an SMS text message with a tracking link, someone entered your number when requesting a Courier trip. Since your number was entered as either the sender or recipient of the package, you can use the information in the text message to coordinate with the driver and track the status of the package delivery.

You can cancel the SMS messages at any time by texting “STOP.” From there you will receive one more message to confirm that you have been unsubscribed. After this, you will no longer receive SMS messages from Uber.

Weight, dimensions and value limits for Uber Courier

Uber Courier is a package delivery service that allows eligible drivers and couriers to earn by transporting items requested through the Uber app. Similar to a trip request, couriers can choose whether to accept or decline delivery requests and are responsible for safely transporting packages from the sender to the recipient. To help ensure a smooth and safe delivery experience, packages must meet specific size, weight, value and safety requirements. The tables below outlines the maximum weight, dimension and value limits for Uber Courier deliveries.

Uber Hotels

Booking a hotel

What is Uber Hotels?

Uber Hotels is a new service to search, book, and pay for hotels directly through the Uber app. Exclusive benefits will also be available for users who have an active Uber One membership. Currently, to book an Uber Hotel you must be located in the United States or Canada.

How can I book a hotel?

To access Uber Hotels, open the Uber app and select the Hotel icon under Services to begin browsing for and booking Hotels.

To book an Uber Hotel, select your desired location, days you are staying, and your number of travelers to begin browsing hotels that are able to accommodate your visit. Once you find a room that meets your preferences, you can book it directly from the Uber App.

Payment methods and policy

You can pay for an Uber Hotels booking using a credit or debit card just like you do for rides in the Uber app. Uber Hotels will pass your preferred payment details to our third-party hotel supplier to process the transaction. Your use of the Uber Hotels booking service is subject to the Uber Hotel Terms and standard Uber policies.

Pricing

Pricing for Uber Hotels varies by property, and can be viewed directly in app.

Can we help with anything else?

FAQs

Uber Hotel FAQ

Can I book more than 1 room with Uber Hotels?

Currently, you can only book 1 room per Uber Hotel booking at this time. You can book individual bookings if you need to make multiple bookings.

Why did my hotel search return no results?

This occurs if no hotels are available for your specific search criteria. Please adjust your search criteria such as your party size and travel dates to view available hotels.

Does Uber support booking home rentals or other property types?

No, currently Uber only offers hotel bookings, but may offer other types of properties in the future.

Is there a fee when booking Hotels through Uber?

Uber does not charge additional booking fees, but hotels may charge taxes or fees, including at the property.

I have a question not listed here. Can I get more help?

For additional assistance regarding Uber Hotels, please contact support at 1-800-232-7277.

Can we help with anything else?

Reviewing and cancelling bookings

Hotels bookings and confirmations

You can view your upcoming, ongoing, and past Hotel bookings in Uber Hotels and your booking confirmation can be found in a confirmation email as well as the Activity Hub section of your Uber app. Your receipt for your Uber Hotel booking will be sent along with the confirmation email, and can be viewed in the booking details page directly from the app.

Canceling your booking

To cancel your booking:

  1. Go to Uber Hotels in your Uber app

  2. Select Reservations at the top of the page

  3. Select the booking you’d like to cancel. Please keep in mind that you will only be able to cancel eligible bookings before your check in time. Further information can be found in the cancellation policy section of your booking.

Getting a refund on your booking

To receive a refund on your booking, you must book a refundable room. Not all rooms are refundable. You can see whether your booking is refundable at the time of booking or when you cancel.

I have a booking question not listed here. Can I get more help?

For any other questions about your Uber Hotel booking, please contact support at 1-800-232-7277

Uber One booking benefits

Uber One members in the United States and Canada earn 10% back in Uber One credits on eligible hotel bookings. To qualify for Uber One Credits from your Uber Hotels booking, you must have an active Uber One membership. You can learn more about Uber One here.

With Uber Hotels, Uber One credits are typically issued around check-in, but may be delayed depending on booking type and partner confirmation. These Uber One credits will require an active Uber One membership for redemption and will expire after a year.

Please keep in mind that benefits and rewards for the Uber Hotels product are only eligible on the Uber platform and are not eligible for other loyalty programs. Hotel loyalty accounts can not be linked at this time.

Uber Shuttle Airports

Airport Shuttle FAQ

What is Shuttle?

Uber Shuttle is a service that runs to and from airports between designated pick-up and drop-off locations. You can reserve from 3 to 7 days before your travel date for yourself and up to five other passengers to travel in a high-quality, safe shuttle. You can book up to 2 minutes before the scheduled departure time.

All riders 2 years or older require a ticket and to be seated with a seat belt. Up to one rider per guardian under the age of 2 may travel for free. Riders must be 18 years of age, unless accompanied by an adult.

Ticket Fare Structure

Uber Shuttle fare is based on the pickup, dropoff, time of booking, time of travel, and number of seats you select plus relevant taxes (if applied in your city).

You can view the fare breakdown in your Uber app when booking.

Payment for your Shuttle must be made via the Uber app. Cash is not accepted on board the Shuttle, and it is not possible to board the Shuttle without prior reservation.

Luggage allowance on Shuttle

The below allowance is included in your ticket’s fare:

Each rider is permitted to bring on board: * One piece of luggage (standard carry-on) recommended dimension 22x14x9 inches weighing up to 45lbs/20.5kg. * One personal item which must fit on your lap or under your seat.

To bring additional luggage, you must book extra seats for each additional item. This helps our drivers accommodate you better and prevents any issues during the trip.

Booking trips

You can view schedules and seat availability in the Uber app. Up to 5 seats can be reserved from 3 to 7 days in advance. By booking in advance, you ensure your seat is reserved for your trip. Seats can fill up fast, so be sure to reserve early!

To book a shuttle in the Uber app

You can book Uber Shuttle 2 ways:

From the Home tab:

  1. Enter your destination in Where to?
  2. Select the Uber Shuttle option.
  3. Select your preferred pickup time and how many seats you need (up to 5 per booking).
  4. Tap Confirm to reserve your Shuttle.

From the Services tab:

  1. Select the Shuttle icon.
  2. Enter your destination.
  3. Select your preferred pickup time and how many seats you need (up to 5 per booking).
  4. Tap Confirm to reserve your Shuttle.
Once you make a reservation in the app
  • You’ll receive a ticket with a QR code and a unique PIN 5 minutes before the trip starts.
  • Your driver will scan your code as you board the shuttle.
  • You can track your shuttle and view the pickup location on the Uber app’s home screen 5 minutes before your scheduled pickup time. The pickup location will be shown on the map.
Editing your booking

To modify shuttle bookings, you’ll need to cancel your scheduled trip and book a new one. You will not be charged for shuttle cancellations.

To cancel a booking
  1. Go to Activity in the Uber app.
  2. Select the trip you want to cancel.
  3. Cancel the reservation.
  4. You may then make a new reservation by selecting the Shuttle icon on the home screen of the Uber App.

If you can’t make your booking, please cancel as soon as possible to open your seat for someone else.

Trip or booking issues

Forgot to book a seat?

You can make a reservation up to 2 minutes before departure, as long as the shuttle has seats available.

Finding routes, stops and scheduled times

All available routes, stops, and scheduled times can be found in the Shuttle section of the Uber app.

To view routes and schedules in the app:

  1. Select the Shuttle icon on the Uber Home screen.
  2. Enter your pickup and drop-off locations.
  3. You’ll see a list of all available routes between your locations.
Cancellations or Delays

Uber Shuttle operates on a frequent schedule, and while cancellations or delays are rare, they can occur due to unforeseen circumstances. Please check the app for the next available Shuttle.

Shuttle Breakdown

Your security is always the priority, and we aim to provide an experience where users always feel safe. In case of a breakdown, we recommend checking the app for the next available shuttle or considering alternative transportation options if needed.

Wheelchair Accessible Vehicles

If you want to request a WAV vehicle, please request the Fleet Partner’s phone number via Uber support.

The Fleet Partner’s phone number can also be found in the Uber app. Schedule your trip at least 24 hours in advance.The Fleet Partner will confirm the time and availability for you to be picked up – from your Uber app, book the designated pickup time the Fleet Partner tells you to book.

Animal/Pet on Shuttle?

  • Service animals are permitted to travel on board the Shuttle.
  • Pets are not allowed on board.

How do I contact customer support?

If you need assistance with lost items, report a bug, or give feedback or suggestions for improving the Uber Shuttle contact Uber support by tapping Help > Uber Shuttle > Airport Shuttle support or scanning the QR code below:

Uber Shuttle Events

Events Shuttle FAQ

Uber Shuttle is a service that runs on schedules between designated pick up and drop off locations. You can book your seat in the Uber app on a high-quality shuttle to enjoy a comfortable, safe, and convenient ride

Trip Price

Fares for Uber Shuttle are based on the route and number of seats you select. Fares are fixed and are not subject to surge pricing.

Payments

Use the Uber app to book and pay for your trip. Cash or other payment methods outside the Uber app are not accepted

How to add a promo code to your account
  1. Tap the profile icon at the bottom of the main screen.
  2. Tap Payment
  3. Choose Promotions
  4. Enter code and tap Add promo

You may find a promo is already added to your account after following these steps. This is because some promo codes add to your account automatically.

Booking trips

You can easily see if seats are available in the app. Trips can be booked up to 7 days in advance. By booking in advance, you ensure your seat is secured for your trip. Seats can fill up fast, so be sure to reserve early!

To book a shuttle in the Uber app
  1. Enter your destination
  2. Select the Uber Shuttle option
  3. Review your fare
  4. Select your preferred pickup time and how many seats you desire (up to 5 per booking)
  5. Tap Book Your Shuttle.
Once you make a reservation in the app

  • You’ll receive a confirmation ticket with a QR code and backup PIN. Scan the code as you board the shuttle.

  • You can track your shuttle and view the pickup location on the home screen of the Uber app starting 25 minutes before your scheduled pickup time. You’ll see the pickup location shown on the map.

If your QR code is not loading, you can give a displayed PIN code to the driver or staff. You may also contact Uber via in-app chat support.

Editing and canceling bookings

To modify shuttle bookings you’ll need to cancel your scheduled trip and book a new one. Please cancel your booking as soon as possible to open your seat for someone else if you can’t make it on time. You may cancel your booking up to 10 minutes prior to your scheduled departure time and receive a full refund. Bookings canceled within 10 minutes of your scheduled departure time are subject to 50% of the total fare as a fee. “No show” bookings are subject to the full fare.

To cancel a booking in the Uber app: 1. Go to Activity in the Uber app. 2. Select the trip you want to cancel. 3. Cancel the reservation.

You can then make a new reservation.

If you have questions about cancellations, please contact Uber via in-app chat support.

Trip or booking issues

Forgot to book a seat?

You can make a reservation at the time of boarding, as long as the shuttle has seats available.

Finding routes, stops and scheduled times

All available routes, stops, and scheduled times can be found in the Shuttle section of the Uber app.

To view routes and schedules in the app:

  1. Select the Uber shuttle on the main screen.
  2. Enter your pickup and drop off locations.
  3. You’ll see a list of all available routes between your locations.

Have feedback or suggestions?

Please let us know! Contact Uber via in-app chat support to give feedback or suggestions for improving the Uber shuttle.

Lost items

If you need assistance with lost items or any other issues, please contact Uber via in-app chat support.

Does the shuttle have wifi?

The shuttle does not offer wifi.

Can I bring an animal/pet on the shuttle?

Only service animals are allowed on Uber Shuttles. Pets aren’t allowed.

Can I drink or eat on the shuttle?

Drinks and food aren’t allowed.

Need to report a bug or technical issue?

Please submit an in app bug report and contact Uber via in-app support. Thank you for reporting these to us.

We value your feedback and are committed to making Shuttle a better experience for you. If you have any suggestions or encounter any issues, please share them with us. We will be happy to improve your experience.

Using TalkBack and VoiceOver

Contact your driver with TalkBack

The options to call or message your driver appear in the white bar at the bottom of your screen once you’ve been matched.

There is a phone icon and a message input box in the white bar where driver’s name and vehicle information appear.

To call your driver:

  1. Single tap, then double tap the round phone icon under your driver’s information
  2. Single tap, then double tap the phone number or the “FREE CALL” button
  3. Your phone will automatically call your driver according to the option you chose

To message your driver:

  1. Single tap, then double tap the text field under your driver’s information
  2. Type or speak your message
  3. Single tap, then double tap the “SEND” button

I have feedback about VoiceOver or TalkBack features

If you have feedback regarding VoiceOver or TalkBack within the Uber app, please let us know here. If you have feedback about the VoiceOver or Talkback features, please contact Apple or Google support.

Navigate the menu with TalkBack

To navigate the app menu with TalkBack, open your app and double tap the top left of your screen to open the menu. The following options appear from top to bottom:

Your trips - This section lists your past rides, or you can see upcoming, scheduled rides. Use two fingers to swipe through your ride history. Select one to get help with a trip, submit feedback, or view a trip receipt.

Payment - in this section you can add or update a payment method

Help - search through articles to answer your questions about the app. You can also submit feedback about accessibility features in this section

Free rides - use the personal Invite code stored in this section to invite others to use the app

Settings - Update your contact information, set favorite locations like home or work, or add contacts to quickly share your location with before or during a trip

CONTACT SUPPORT

If you have accessibility related questions or feedback, you can submit this under “Accessibility” in the “Help” section of the menu. Report any other trip related issue by selecting the specific trip from the “Your Trips” section of the menu. From the resulting screen, you can choose from a variety of options to report an issue.

Navigate the menu with VoiceOver

To navigate the app menu with VoiceOver, open your app and double tap the top left of your screen to open the menu. The following options appear from top to bottom:

Your trips - this section lists your past rides, or you can see upcoming, scheduled rides. Use three fingers to swipe through your ride history. Select one to get help with a trip, submit feedback, or view a trip receipt.

Wallet - in this section you can add or update a payment method

Help - search through articles to answer your questions. You can also submit feedback about accessibility features in this section.

Free rides - use the personal Invite code stored in this section to invite others to use the app

Settings - update your contact information, set favorite locations like home or work, or add contacts to quickly share your location with before or during a trip

CONTACT SUPPORT

If you have accessibility-related questions or feedback, you can contact us by tapping “Accessibility” in the “Help” section of the menu. Report any other trip-related issue by selecting the specific trip from the “Your Trips” section of the menu. From the next screen, you can choose from a variety of options to report an issue.

Request a ride with TalkBack

Here’s how to request a ride with TalkBack turned on:

  1. Single tap, then double tap the “Where to?” box. Enter a destination, or select from a list of suggested destinations.
  2. Your pickup location is automatically set to your GPS location. To change it, tap your pickup location, then double tap it to edit your location.
  3. Use two fingers to swipe through the vehicle options available in your area. Single tap, then double tap one to select it for your ride.
  4. Single tap, then double tap the “CONFIRM PICKUP” button at the bottom of your screen to request your ride
  5. You’ll receive a notification when your driver has arrived. The message will be read aloud.
  6. Single tap, then double tap the bar at the bottom of your screen to access the following options:
  • Contact your driver - Single tap, then double tap the round phone icon call your driver’s phone number or place a free call
  • Cancel your ride - Single tap, then double tap the “Cancel” button, then single tap, then double tap the “Yes, cancel” button to confirm.
  • Share you status - Single tap, then double tap the “Share Status” button, then select people from your list of contacts. Uber texts a link to those contacts to track your ride.
  • Split the fare - Single tap, then double tap the “Split Fare” button and select a contact

Zipcar on Uber

After your trip ends

Please note that some circumstances may require that we redirect you to Zipcar for support including:

  • Issues with adding trip photos at dropoff
  • Lost Items
  • Parking Concerns

If you are experiencing any of these scenarios, please contact Zipcar directly at 1 (866)- 494-7227. An Uber representative will not be able to assist with these issues at this time. . You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

I haven’t seen the promotion apply to my account yet (e.g. Uber Cash)

Promotions and Uber Cash are typically applied to your account within 7 days after the rental car was returned. If it has been less than 7 days, the cashback should show up in the next few days. If you believe there have been any errors in applying promotions to your account, or if you have additional questions about the promotions, please contact us below.

I lost an item in my Zipcar

In order to retrieve a missing item, you will need to contact Zipcar directly at 1 (866)- 494-7227 and they can assist you with locating your lost items.

I found an item in my Zipcar

If you’ve found an item in the Zipcar after your trip or while starting your reservation, please leave the item in the trunk of the car if possible. This ensures it remains secure for the owner or the previous renter. If the item appears hazardous or prevents safe use of the vehicle, call Zipcar Support for further instructions.

I am having issues with ending my trip in the App

If you are having issues with ending the trip:

  • Make sure you’re parked at your designated return location.
  • Check your phone’s signal/Bluetooth and move closer to the car if needed.
  • Close and reopen the Uber app, then try again.
  • If prompted, upload the required photos and complete the checklist to finish ending your trip.

If the app still won’t let you end the trip:

  • Chat with an Uber agent below.
  • If you’re blocked and can’t leave the car unlocked or safely parked, call Zipcar Support at 1 (866)- 494-7227 for immediate assistance.

Parking issue on return

If your designated parking space is blocked or unclear, call Zipcar Support for guidance at 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

I was overcharged

Post-booking, you may see charges on your payment method up to 2 weeks after your trip ends. These can include:

  • Late return fees
  • Low fuel fee
  • Mileage overage
  • Vehicle damage charges
  • Tolls and violations

If you believe a charge is incorrect, please contact Zipcar directly at 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

Before you book

What is Zipcar on Uber?

Zipcar is a flexible car-sharing service you can book by the hour or day directly in the Uber app. Cars are parked in convenient neighborhood locations and can be unlocked with your phone. Booking through Uber includes:

  • Flexible rental periods & hourly rentals
  • No membership fees or application fees
  • Transparent pricing with free gas/charging included
  • Every Zipcar trip includes 150 miles per day
  • Ability to see the exact car before booking
  • Keyless entry
  • 10% cashback on eligible rental for Uber One members

Who can Rent a Zipcar?

Anyone who is 25 years or older with a valid U.S. or Canadian driver’s license and in good account standing can book a Zipcar on Uber.

What kind of cars do you offer through Zipcar?

Car availability varies by region, but generally Zipcar has Electric Vehicles, Cargo Vans, Minivans, Trucks, Sedans and SUVs.

How do I book with Zipcar?

Open the Uber app and go to Rental Cars from your home screen or the Services tab. Enter your search criteria (such as location, dates, and times) and run the search. Please note that a Zipcar rental must be returned to the same location it is picked up from. If you have a different drop off location in your search criteria, you will not see Zipcar as an option. Please adjust your search criteria and search again.

On the results page, scroll through the available options to find a car that suits your needs. If you only want to see Zipcar vehicles, apply the Rental companies filter and select Zipcar.

After you’ve selected the Zipcar that meets your needs, proceed to checkout.

Once your payment goes through, you will be prompted to do an ID verification where you upload your driver’s license and take a selfie. You will need a valid U.S. or Canadian license and the selfie must match the license.

After your driver’s license is verified and you’re confirmed to be over 25, Zipcar will run an additional identity and risk check. This is usually completed within minutes, but in some cases it can take up to three days for approval.

Insurance and protection options

Zipcar offers the following protection options that you can add on as part of your booking under the ‘car details’ page:

  • Combined protection: covers third party liability and all car damage fees for damage done to the Zipcar. Damage protection does not cover damage done to other vehicles or structures. You can view Zipcar’s help center for basic insurance protection coverage here
  • Liability protection: third party liability coverage up to $300,000. You can view Zipcar’s help center for liability protection coverage here
  • Damage protection: covers all car damage fees. You can view Zipcar’s help center for damage protection coverage here
  • Decline damage protection: $2,500 deductible may apply in case of any damage to the vehicle. Your personal insurance and credit card may not cover this trip.

Zipcar Availability Issues

If you cannot find any available Zipcars, this may be due to several factors such as local product availability, your personal eligibility for Zipcar rentals, or the current status of your Zipcar account.

Currently, Zipcar operates in these specific locations, Atlanta, Austin, Baltimore, Boston, Chicago, Dallas, Denver, Detroit, Honolulu, Houston, Los Angeles, Miami, Minneapolis, Milwaukee, New York/New Jersey, Philadelphia, Pittsburgh, Sacramento, San Diego, San Francisco Bay Area, Seattle, Washington D.C., Toronto

If Zipcar is not appearing despite being in one of these cities, you might not meet the necessary rental requirements. For instance, Zipcar requires drivers to be at least 25 years old. If you are under 25, you will need to book through a different rental partner on our app that permits younger drivers. Be sure you select your correct age when booking a rental to ensure eligible rentals are surfaced to you.

If you are in an active service area and meet all the eligibility criteria but still face availability issues, please reach out to Zipcar directly at 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

During Your Trip

I can’t find my car

If you’re unable to locate your reserved Zipcar:

  • Check the pickup location shown in your reservation details — sometimes it’s in a parking garage or a designated spot.
  • Make sure you’ve looked for signage, parking space numbers, or notes in the app about where the car is parked.
  • Press the “honk/horn” button inside the app to honk the horn on the car, which can help you to locate your rental.
  • If the app shows the car’s location but you still can’t find it, try refreshing your reservation page for updated details.
  • If the vehicle appears to be missing or the location seems incorrect, call Zipcar Support at 1 (866)- 494-7227 right away for assistance.

An Uber agent will not be able to move or locate the vehicle for you; Zipcar will have the most accurate, real-time car location data and can help resolve the issue.

Fuel & EV Charging

For gas vehicles:

  • Fuel is included in your Zipcar booking. Each car has a fuel card inside (usually in the visor or glove box).
  • When using the card, if you are prompted to input a zipcode, please enter your billing zipcode
  • If the tank is below ¼ when you return, please refill it using the card. Instructions for using the card are in the car and in your reservation details.
  • If you fail to return the vehicle with at least ¼th a tank of gas, you will be charged a fee.

If you are having any other issues with the fuel card, you can visit the Zipcar help center for additional assistance here.

For electric vehicles (EVs):

  • EVs should be returned with at least the same charge level you started with.
  • You can use the provided charging card (if available in the car) or public charging stations.
  • Plug the car back in at the home location if a charger is available.

If the fuel card or charging card is missing, doesn’t work, or you can’t charge the EV, contact Zipcar Support for help by calling 1 (866)- 494-7227.

Help With Pickup

Please note that the following scenarios may require reaching out to Zipcar directly:

  • Issues with lock/unlock on your rental beyond basic troubleshooting
  • Issues with a damaged/dirty car
  • Mechanical Issues/car won’t start

If you are experiencing one of the above scenarios, please contact Zipcar directly by calling 1 (866)- 494-7227. An Uber representative will not be able to assist with these issues at this time. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

How do I lock/unlock my car?

You can lock and unlock your Zipcar directly in the Uber app — no key or card needed:

  1. Make sure that the Bluetooth is activated and that the Uber app is allowed to access the bluetooth functionality in your application settings.
  2. Head towards your pick up location.
  3. Open the Uber app → Rental Cars.
  4. Click on your reservation details through the upcoming reservation screen under “Manage reservation”
  5. Tap “Start Trip” and follow the checklist to capture a few photos from different angles and document the car’s condition.
  6. Starting your trip will unlock the car doors. During the trip, you can lock or unlock the car at any time using the lock and unlock buttons in the app.

If the car doesn’t respond after a few tries, call Zipcar Support 1 (866)- 494-7227 for further assistance. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

Bluetooth isn’t working

If the app can’t connect via Bluetooth to start or unlock the car:

  1. Move closer to the car and make sure your phone’s Bluetooth is turned on.
  2. Close and reopen the Uber app, then try again.

If you’re locked out or unable to start your trip, call Zipcar Support at 1 (866)- 494-7227 for immediate help.

My car is damaged or dirty

If your Zipcar has visible damage, is dirty, or has low fuel when you arrive:

  • Start the trip and then take clear photos of the issue (you’ll be prompted in the app to confirm the car’s condition).
  • Report the issue during the pickup photo checklist if possible.
  • If the damage looks unsafe to drive or prevents you from using the car:

Call Zipcar Support at 1 (866)- 494-7227 immediately for guidance or to request a replacement.

There is a mechanical issue / My car won’t start

If your Zipcar won’t start or has a mechanical issue:

  1. Make sure you’ve tapped Start trip in the Uber app.
  2. Check the dashboard for any messages (e.g., low fuel, dead battery).
  3. Try locking and unlocking the car in the app once more.

If the car still won’t start:

  • Call Zipcar Support immediately at 1 (866)- 494-7227 — they can troubleshoot or arrange help.
  • If it’s unsafe or you’re stranded, call emergency services first.

I’m having trouble adding pickup photos

If you’re unable to upload the required pickup photos in the app:

  • Check that you have a strong cell signal or Wi-Fi.
  • Ensure the Uber app has permission to use your camera/photos (check phone settings).
  • Try closing and reopening the app or retaking the photos in better lighting.

If you still can’t upload photos, please contact Zipcar support at 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

My reservation was cancelled/I don’t want the new car

Occasionally, Zipcar may cancel or move your reservation if the original vehicle becomes unavailable (for example, due to damage, maintenance needs, or the previous renter not returning it on time). When this happens, Zipcar will try to rebook you into a comparable vehicle nearby.

If your reservation has been moved or cancelled and the replacement car is too far or doesn’t work for you, you can check your updated reservation details in the Uber app for the new car and pickup location.

If the replacement doesn’t meet your needs, call Zipcar Support at 1 (866)- 494-7227 to request another option or to cancel without penalty.

Uber agents can’t override Zipcar’s vehicle substitutions, but Zipcar Support can help find alternatives or cancel the trip. We recommend calling them at 1 (866)- 494-7227.

I have a safety issue

I am experiencing a safety issue (i.e. mechanical issues, I got into an accident)

If you experience a safety issue during your trip, your immediate safety is the top priority. If you’re in danger or need urgent assistance, please call emergency services right away.

For any safety-related concerns with the vehicle or the host (including mechanical issues or if you’ve been in an accident), you’ll need to contact Zipcar immediately so they can assist with resolving the issue and ensure your safety. They can guide you through the next steps and help determine the best course of action. An Uber agent will not be able to assist you with a Zipcar related safety concern.

Call 1 (866) 494-7227 to get in contact with Zipcar now.

Can we help with anything else?

Managing an existing reservation

Modify or cancel after confirmation

Once your Zipcar booking is confirmed, any changes (modifications or cancellations) must be handled by Zipcar Support directly.

If you need to cancel your upcoming booking:

  1. Go to your reservation in the Uber app.
  2. Scroll down and tap Cancel Reservation.
  3. Select a cancellation reason and confirm.

After the free cancellation window, Zipcar may charge a cancellation fee (shown before you confirm). You can learn more about Zipcar’s cancellation policy here.

I want to shorten my booking

Once your Zipcar booking has been confirmed, any changes—including shortening your trip or adjusting your end time—must be handled directly by Zipcar Support.

Because Zipcar manages the vehicle fleet and real-time availability, Uber cannot modify the duration of a confirmed reservation on their behalf.

To get in touch with Zipcar, contact them at 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

I want to extend my booking

Once your Zipcar booking has been confirmed, any requests to extend your trip must be handled directly by Zipcar Support.

Because Zipcar manages vehicle availability and bookings for all members, Uber isn’t able to modify the duration of an existing reservation. You can extend your booking only if the vehicle hasn’t already been reserved by another user during the additional time you’d like to keep it.

To get in touch with Zipcar, contact them at 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

I want to cancel my reservation/How to cancel

In order to cancel your booking in the Uber App, please use the following steps:

  1. Go to your Reservation in the Uber app.
  2. Scroll down and tap Cancel Reservation.
  3. Select a cancellation reason and confirm.

If you’d prefer to cancel with an agent, you can contact Zipcar support directly at 1 (866)- 494-7227 to process the cancellation. Uber agents cannot cancel a reservation for you.

Please note that any cancellations may be subject to a cancellation fee, as outlined in the Zipcars cancellation policy here.

ID Verification Failed

If your driver’s license or ID verification did not go through, it’s usually due to one of the following:

  • The photo was blurry or had glare, making the text unreadable.
  • The picture didn’t clearly match your face (e.g., hats, sunglasses, filters).
  • The document details (name, DOB, address) didn’t match your Uber account.

Tips to resolve:

  • Retake the photo in good lighting with no glare or shadows.
  • Hold the camera steady and make sure the entire document is visible.
  • Remove hats or glasses when taking your selfie so it matches your ID.

If you’ve tried resubmitting and your ID still can’t be verified, click here to chat with an Uber agent for help with next steps. In some cases, your request may need to be escalated for manual review.

My payment won’t go through/My booking failed

If your Zipcar booking doesn’t complete or your payment is declined, it’s often because:

  • The card on file is not an accepted payment method (Zipcar requires either a valid debit card or credit card, and does not accept prepaid cards of any kind.).
  • There’s an issue with your bank (e.g., insufficient funds, declined authorization).
  • The card details on your Uber account are outdated or incorrect.

Tips to resolve:

  1. Open the Uber app and go to Wallet → Payment Methods.
  2. Make sure a valid credit card (not debit or prepaid) is added and set as the default.
  3. Retry the booking.
  4. If prompted, complete ID verification with a clear photo of your valid U.S. or Canadian license and a selfie that matches the photo.
  5. Contact your bank if the charge was declined for security reasons.

If your payment still fails after updating your card, please contact Zipcar directly by calling 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

For more details on booking with Zipcar, see Zipcar FAQs.

Zipcar FAQs

If your question is not answered here, please visit support.zipcar.com for help. In some cases, contacting Zipcar support directly will be necessary.

What payment methods do you take?

For Zipcar reservations, we accept valid debit cards and credit cards only (including American Express). We do NOT accept Uber Credits, PayPal, Apple Pay, Google Pay, or Prepaid cards. We also do not accept any financed payments (e.g. Klarna, Affirm, etc). If you need to pay with one of these methods, please select a different rental type in the app.

Insurance and protection options

Zipcar offers the following protection options that you can add on as part of your booking under the ‘car details’ page:

  • Combined protection: covers third party liability and all car damage fees for damage done to the Zipcar. Damage protection does not cover damage done to other vehicles or structures. You can view Zipcar’s help center for basic insurance protection coverage here
  • Liability protection: third party liability coverage up to $300,000. You can view Zipcar’s help center for liability protection coverage here
  • Damage protection: covers all car damage fees. You can view Zipcar’s help center for damage protection coverage here
  • Decline damage protection: $2,500 deductible may apply in case of any damage to the vehicle. Your personal insurance and credit card may not cover this trip.

Mileage & Rental Limits

Zipcar bookings include 150 miles per day. Additional miles are charged at a per-mile rate. Multi-day bookings are allowed if vehicles are available.

Deposit Information

Zipcar currently does not charge deposits. The total cost you see at checkout is what you will be charged. Keep in mind, your booking may be subject to post-trip charges, such as tolls or violations.

How do I cancel my trip?

To cancel booking in the Uber App:

  1. Go to your Reservation in the Uber app.
  2. Scroll down and tap Cancel Reservation.
  3. Select a cancellation reason and confirm.

If you’d prefer to cancel with an agent, you can contact Zipcar support directly at 1 (866)- 494-7227 to process the cancellation. Uber agents cannot cancel a reservation for you.

Please note that any cancellations may be subject to a cancellation fee, as outlined in the Zipcars cancellation policy here.

Cancellation Policy for Zipcar on Uber

  • If your trip starts within 30 min of booking → cancel free until the trip starts.
  • If your trip is 8 hrs or less → cancel free up to 3 hrs before start or within 30 min of booking (whichever is later).
  • If your trip is longer than 8 hrs → cancel free up to 24 hrs before start or within 30 min of booking (whichever is later).

After the free window ends, a cancellation fee may apply. The app will show the fee before you confirm.

If you would like to modify your upcoming or active booking, please contact Zipcar support. For upcoming bookings, you can also cancel the booking and rebook. Please note, cancellations may be subject to a cancellation fee. For additional questions, please contact Zipcar support at 1 (866)- 494-7227. You can also chat with Zipcar here by selecting the chat icon in the bottom right hand corner of the webpage.

How do I pick up the car and start my trip?

  1. Head to the pickup location shown in your reservation.
  2. Open the Uber app and go to Rental Cars (from your home screen or the Services tab).
  3. Tap your upcoming reservation → Manage car → Start trip → Unlock.

Follow the prompts to:

  1. Take a few required photos of the car from different angles (the app will guide you).
  2. Complete a quick pre-trip checklist.
  3. Agree to a few driving and rental rules.

Once these steps are done, your Zipcar will unlock and you’re ready to drive.

How do I unlock the car?

You can unlock your Zipcar right from the Uber app — no key or card needed:

  1. Open the Uber app and go to Rental Cars (from your home screen or the Services tab).
  2. Tap your upcoming reservation → Manage car → Start trip → Unlock.
  3. Wait a few seconds while the app connects to the car and unlocks it.

Make sure you have enabled Bluetooth on your device before you attempt to unlock the vehicle. If you’re having trouble, move closer to the car and try again.

If the car doesn’t unlock after a few tries, you can chat with an Uber agent for help or call Zipcar Support if it’s an urgent on-site issue.

How do I end the trip?

  1. Return to your drop-off location on time. Please remember that the drop off location MUST be the same as the pickup location. No exceptions.
  2. Plan to arrive with enough time left in your booking. Late returns can lead to penalties such as incurring late fees
  3. In the Uber app, go to Rental Cars → open your active reservation → tap End trip.

Follow the prompts to:

  1. Upload photos of the car from different angles. These protect you from future damage claims and help Zipcar keep vehicles clean and well maintained.
  2. Confirm any final checklist items.

Please note that If you end your trip early, you won’t receive a prorated refund for unused time. If you return the car early but don’t end your trip, you’ll continue to have access — and be billed — until your scheduled booking time ends.

Can we help with anything else?
